## INTRODUCTION - FITTINGS  [939011391]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > INTRODUCTION - FITTINGS
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011391

#### WARNINGS
Work carefully on the fittings and use the recommended tools correctly to prevent damage.

#### PRECAUTIONS
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.

## INTRODUCTION - PASSENGER COMPARTMENT FRONT SIDE DOORS  [939011153]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7005 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT FRONT SIDE DOORS > INTRODUCTION - PASSENGER COMPARTMENT FRONT SIDE DOORS
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011153

#### WARNINGS
Observe the following precautions when working on the front door assembly:
- When working on the hinges, take care not to damage the paintwork.
- Following a door adjustment operation that required the hinges to be moved, the door must be removed and the hinge area repainted.
- Work carefully on the fittings and use the recommended tools correctly to prevent damage.

#### PRECAUTIONS
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.

## 7005A08 INTERIOR SUPPORT FRAME FOR ONE SIDE DOOR - R.R. WITH DOOR PANEL REMOVED  [939011109]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7005 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT FRONT SIDE DOORS > 7005A FRONT DOOR ASSEMBLY > 7005A08 INTERIOR SUPPORT FRAME FOR ONE SIDE DOOR - R.R. WITH DOOR PANEL REMOVED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011109

##### Removing
Op. 7005D30 FRONT SIDE DESCENDING WINDOW GLASS SCRAPER (INNER) (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011132]
Op. 7005D15 FRONT SIDE DOOR DESCENDING WINDOW GLASS (ONE) LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R [→939011128]
Op. 7005C17 FRONT SIDE DOOR LOCK BARREL - R.R. [→939011122]
1. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| DRIVER'S DOOR JUNCTION UNIT | See M066 DRIVER'S DOOR CONTROL UNIT (NPG) [→939004346] |
| PASSENGER SIDE DOOR JUNCTION UNIT | See M067 PASSENGER DOOR CONTROL UNIT (NPP) [→939004363] |
2. Disconnect retainers (2a) of rear view mirror wiring (2b) from the chassis.
3. Lift ring nut (3a) and disconnect the door electrical connection (3b).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| REAR JOINT/FRONT DOOR DRIVER SIDE | See D030 DRIVER'S FRONT DOOR COUPLING [→939003890] |
| REAR JOINT/FRONT DOOR PASSENGER SIDE | See D031 PASSENGER FRONT DOOR COUPLING [→939003900] |
4. Release the cable duct (4a) from the doro frame opening (4b).
5. Cut the door wiring retaining band.
6. Push the electrical connection into the door frame as far as possible.
7. Undo the bolts fixing the support frame.
8. Loosen the lock fixing bolts.
9. Move support frame (9a) slightly from the front side and remove connection (9b) from the door frame.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001865.jpg]

1. Disconnect link (1a) form the door frame (1b), undoing retainers (1c) and (1d).
2. Recover the support frame.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001866.jpg]

- If necessary carry out
Op. 7205E37 STRIPPING FRONT SIDE DOOR INTERIOR SUPPORT FRAME [→939011435]

##### Refitting
- Check that the support frame is not damaged.
- If necessary carry out
Op. 7205E37 STRIPPING FRONT SIDE DOOR INTERIOR SUPPORT FRAME [→939011435]
1. Before positioning support frame (1a), check that the perimeter seal (1b) is undamaged and clean.
Also check that the contact surface on the door frame is clean.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001867.jpg]

1. Position the support frame.
2. Connect link (2a) to door frame (2b), doing up retainers (2c) and (2d).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001868.jpg]

1. Fit connection (1a) into the door frame so that it emerges from opening (1b).
2. Apply collar (2a) to the wiring to reduce free length.
Check that wiring (2b) does not interfere with glass movement.
3. Position the link from outer handle to lock (3a) as shown in the figure.
Before fitting the lock, check that link (3a) is in the position shown in the figure.
4. Tighten the lock fixing bolts.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DOOR LOCK | Bolt | M6x12 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
5. Place frame (5a) in position and secure it using the bolts (5b).
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DOOR INNER FRAME | Bolt | M6x16 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
6. Fit the wiring duct to the door frame.
7. Connect electrical connection (7a) to the door and secure with ring nut (7b).
8. Disconnect retainers (8a) of rear view mirror wiring (8b) from the chassis.
9. Connect the rear view mirror electrical connection.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001869.jpg]

Op. 7005C17 FRONT SIDE DOOR LOCK BARREL - R.R. [→939011122]
Op. 7005D15 FRONT SIDE DOOR DESCENDING WINDOW GLASS (ONE) LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R [→939011128]
Op. 7005D30 FRONT SIDE DESCENDING WINDOW GLASS SCRAPER (INNER) (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011132]

## 7005A10 FRONT SIDE DOOR - ADJUST STRIKER AND ADAPT SEAL IF NECESSARY  [939011111]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7005 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT FRONT SIDE DOORS > 7005A FRONT DOOR ASSEMBLY > 7005A10 FRONT SIDE DOOR - ADJUST STRIKER AND ADAPT SEAL IF NECESSARY
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011111

Check that the rear door is aligned, otherwise:
Op. 7010A10 REAR SIDE DOOR - ADJUST STRIKER AND ADAPT SEAL IF NECESSARY [→939011158]
Open the front door.
1. Check that the door seal is not damaged along its perimeter, otherwise replace.
2. Check that the upper reference (2a) coincides with the middle of angle (2b) and that joint (2c) coincides with the water drain groove (2d). Otherwise prise the seal out of the perimeter housing and refit, observing the markings.
3. If necessary, align bolts (3a) and move lock striker (3b) to the correct position using the slotted bolt holes. The arrows indicate the possible movements for the adjustment.
Tighten the bolts (3a) to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DOOR LOCK STRIKER | Bolt | M8x20 | 2.2÷ 2.6 |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000617.jpg]

- Check that the stiker and the door lock are correctly matched and that the alignment is correct with the door closed.

## 7005A14 FRONT SIDE DOOR - ADJUST CENTERING AND MOULDING - INCLUDES ADAPTATION OF HINGES AND SEALS ON DOOR COMPARTMENT  [939011112]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7005 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT FRONT SIDE DOORS > 7005A FRONT DOOR ASSEMBLY > 7005A14 FRONT SIDE DOOR - ADJUST CENTERING AND MOULDING - INCLUDES ADAPTATION OF HINGES AND SEALS ON DOOR COMPARTMENT
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011112

##### Removing
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DOOR HALF HINGES | Bolt | M6x14 | 1.2 ÷ 1.8 |  |
Op. 7005B14 FRONT SIDE DOOR LOCK STRIKER - R.R [→939011118]
- If necessary, tighten the bolts securing the hinge halves to the door and body.
2. Unscrew opening limiter bolts (2a) and close the door temporarily to return link (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001870.jpg]

- Initially adjust the door within the door compartment. To do this, the bolts securing the hinges to the body must be loosened.
- For the left door upper hinges.
Op. 7040A37 FUSE BOX COVER IN INSTRUMENT PANEL - R.R [→939011243]
Op. 5505A35 MAIN BODY COMPUTER/JUNCTION UNIT - R.R. [→939010967]
- For the right door upper hinges.
Op. 7040A65 CONSOLE SIDE CLOSING (ONE) - R.R. [→939011230]
Op. 7040G20 BULB HOLDER FOR COMPARTMENT LIGHTING IN DASHBOARD - R.R. [→939011260]
Op. 7040A31 DASHBOARD COMPARTMENT ASSEMBLY - R.R. [→939011237]
- For the lower hinges on both doors.
Op. 7040L40 FRONT DOOR RUNNING BOARD (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011274]
1. Loosen the bolts (1a) securing hinge halves (1b) to the body.
2. Adjust the door position by means of the slotted bolt holes until it is correctly aligned in the compartment.
> Note: It is advisable to work on one hinge at a time. The arrows indicate the possible movements for the adjustment.
3. After adjustment, tighten the bolts to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DOOR HINGES TO BODYSHELL | Bolt | M8x30 | 2.9 ÷ 3.5 |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001871.jpg]

- Refit the components removed to access the hinge fixing bolts.
Op. 7040L40 FRONT DOOR RUNNING BOARD (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011274]
Op. 7040A65 CONSOLE SIDE CLOSING (ONE) - R.R. [→939011230]
Op. 7040G20 BULB HOLDER FOR COMPARTMENT LIGHTING IN DASHBOARD - R.R. [→939011260]
Op. 7040A31 DASHBOARD COMPARTMENT ASSEMBLY - R.R. [→939011237]
Op. 7040A37 FUSE BOX COVER IN INSTRUMENT PANEL - R.R [→939011243]
Op. 5505A35 MAIN BODY COMPUTER/JUNCTION UNIT - R.R. [→939010967]
- If necessary, align the door with the wing and rear door as follows.
- Open the door.
1. Loosen the bolts (1a) securing hinges (1b) to the body.
2. Adjust the door position by means of the slotted bolt holes until it is correctly aligned with the wing and rear door.
> Note: It is advisable to work on one hinge at a time. The arrows indicate the possible movements for the adjustment.
3. After adjustment, tighten the bolts to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DOOR HINGES | Bolt | M8x30 | 2.9 ÷ 3.5 |  |
4. Remove link (4a) and tighten bolts (4b) to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DOOR CHECK STRAP | Bolt | M6x22 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001872.jpg]

- Refit and adjust the door striker.
Op. 7005B14 FRONT SIDE DOOR LOCK STRIKER - R.R [→939011118]
- Touch up the paint in the hinge half area.
- Check that the stiker and the door lock are correctly matched and that the alignment is correct with the door closed.

## 7005A28 FRONT SIDE DOOR LIMITER - R.R.  [939011113]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7005 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT FRONT SIDE DOORS > 7005A FRONT DOOR ASSEMBLY > 7005A28 FRONT SIDE DOOR LIMITER - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011113

##### Removing
1. Unscrew the bolts on the door.
2. Unscrew the nuts on the body.
3. Release the seal.
4. Remove the limiter by extracting from inside the car.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000618.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the limiter and the seal are not damaged.
- Place the limiter in its seat.
- Tighten the nuts to the body to the recommended torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DOOR CHECK STRAP | Nut | M6 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
- Fit the seal.
- Tighten the door bolts to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DOOR CHECK STRAP | Bolt | M6x22 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
- Check the operation of the limiter.

## 7005A34 FRONT SIDE DOOR COMPARTMENT SEAL - R.R  [939011114]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7005 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT FRONT SIDE DOORS > 7005A FRONT DOOR ASSEMBLY > 7005A34 FRONT SIDE DOOR COMPARTMENT SEAL - R.R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011114

##### Removing
- Open the door.
1. Release the seal along the entire perimeter of the door housing.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000619.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the seal is not damaged.
1. Fit the seal (1e) in position ensuring that the reference (1a) is aligned with the centre of the corner (1b) and the join (1c) with the water drainage splining (1d).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000620.jpg]

- Check that the door shuts properly and is correctly aligned with the bodywork.

## 7005A46 FRONT SIDE DOOR LOWER SEAL - R.R.  [939011115]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7005 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT FRONT SIDE DOORS > 7005A FRONT DOOR ASSEMBLY > 7005A46 FRONT SIDE DOOR LOWER SEAL - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011115

##### Removing
- Open the door.
1. Release the retaining buttons using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
> Note: When refitting, replace the retaining buttons.
2. Keep the gasket.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000621.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the seal is not damaged.
- Place the seal in position and secure it using new retaining buttons.

## 7005B14 FRONT SIDE DOOR LOCK STRIKER - R.R  [939011118]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7005 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT FRONT SIDE DOORS > 7005B FRONT DOOR MECHANICAL LOCKS > 7005B14 FRONT SIDE DOOR LOCK STRIKER - R.R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011118

##### Removing
1. Open the door and undo the bolts.
2. Keep the striker.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004783.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the striker is not damaged.
1. Position the striker.
2. Screw in the bolts without tightening them.
3. Move the striker into the correct position, using the slotted openings in the bolts. The arrows indicate the possible movements for the adjustment.
4. Tighten the retaining bolts to the recommended torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DOOR LOCK STRIKER | Bolt | M8x20 | 2.2÷ 2.6 |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004784.jpg]

- Check that the stiker and the door lock are correctly matched and that the alignment is correct with the door closed.

- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7005 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT FRONT SIDE DOORS > 7005C FRONT DOOR LOCK CONTROLS

## 7005C17 FRONT SIDE DOOR LOCK BARREL - R.R.  [939011122]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7005 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT FRONT SIDE DOORS > 7005C FRONT DOOR LOCK CONTROLS > 7005C17 FRONT SIDE DOOR LOCK BARREL - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011122

##### Removing
- Open the door.
1. Release the trim from the inner retainers and remove it.
2. Release the rubber trim.
3. Loosen the inner bolt (3a) until the lock assembly (3b) can be released with the key (3c) inserted.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000626.jpg]

1. Release the end (1a) of the retaining clip (1b), then extract it keeping the retainer (1c) pressed.
2. Insert the key (2a) in the lock (2b) and extract it pressing the retainer (1c).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000627.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the lock barrel for the key is not damaged.
1. Insert the lock (1b) with the key (1a) in place, keeping the retainer (1c) pressed.
2. Fit the retaining clip (2a), then atttach the end (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000628.jpg]

1. Using the key (1a), fit the lock barrel (1b), attaching the end (1c) in the housing, then tighten the lock barrel retaining bolt (1d).
2. Fit the rubber trim.
3. Fit the trim attaching the inner retainers.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000629.jpg]

- Check that the lock barrel is working.

## 7005D15 FRONT SIDE DOOR DESCENDING WINDOW GLASS (ONE) LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R  [939011128]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7005 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT FRONT SIDE DOORS > 7005D FRONT DOOR WINDOW GLASSES > 7005D15 FRONT SIDE DOOR DESCENDING WINDOW GLASS (ONE) LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011128

##### Removing
Op. 7005E12 CORNER COVER FOR ONE FRONT SIDE DOOR, LH OR RH - R.R. [→939011137]
Op. 7005E38 FRONT SIDE DOOR INTERIOR HANDLE- R.R. [→939011138]
Op. 7040E24 LOWER COURTESY LIGHT IN DOOR PANEL - R+R [→939011254]
Op. 7005E10 FRONT SIDE DOOR INTERIOR PANEL - R.R. [→939011136]
Op. 7005D30 FRONT SIDE DESCENDING WINDOW GLASS SCRAPER (INNER) (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011132]
1. Open the access covers to the window retaining terminals.
2. Provisionally connect the window switch unit to connection (2a), then move the window until the glass retaining terminals (2b) are in an accessible position (as in the figure).
If the window motor (2c) is faulty, remove and then move the window by inserting a tool in the groove left free by the motor.
3. Loosen bolts (3a) of terminals (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001874.jpg]

1. Proceeding with car, lift the glass by hand (first raise from the rear) and remove from the outside of the door frame.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001875.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check the condition of the glass, retaining terminals and slide channels.
- Position the glass in its housing with care and lower until it is housed in the retaining terminals.
- Ensure the glass is correctly fitted in the terminals, then push home in the rear slide channel.
- Tighten the retaining terminal bolts.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| FRONT DOOR WINDOW | Bolt | M6x16 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
- If removed, fit the window motor.
- Close the access covers to the window retaining terminals.
Provisionally connect the window switch unit to the connection, then move the window to monitor its operation.

## 7005E10 FRONT SIDE DOOR INTERIOR PANEL - R.R.  [939011136]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7005 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT FRONT SIDE DOORS > 7005E FRONT DOOR TRIM > 7005E10 FRONT SIDE DOOR INTERIOR PANEL - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011136

##### Removing
Op. 7005E12 CORNER COVER FOR ONE FRONT SIDE DOOR, LH OR RH - R.R. [→939011137]
Op. 7005E38 FRONT SIDE DOOR INTERIOR HANDLE- R.R. [→939011138]
Op. 7040E24 LOWER COURTESY LIGHT IN DOOR PANEL - R+R [→939011254]
1.Release the trim cover.
2. Undo the bolts.
3. Move the panel (3a) slightly releasing the inner retainers (3b) using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
4. Disconnect the electrical connection (4a) for the anti-theft warning light (4b) (driver's door only).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| ALARM LED | See E040 ALARM LED [→939003978] |
5. Releaase the retainers (5a) for the interior handle (5b).
6. Keep the inner panel.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005160.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the inner panel is not damaged.
- Attach the interior handle retainers.
- Connect the electrical connection for the anti-theft warning light.
- Place the panel in position and attach the inner retainers.
- Tighten the bolts.
- Fit the trim cover.

## 7005E38 FRONT SIDE DOOR INTERIOR HANDLE- R.R.  [939011138]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7005 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT FRONT SIDE DOORS > 7005E FRONT DOOR TRIM > 7005E38 FRONT SIDE DOOR INTERIOR HANDLE- R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011138

##### Removing
1. Remove the trim.
2. Undo the bolts.
3. Lift up the grab handle (3a) slightly releasing the retainers (3b).
4. Disconnect the electrical connection for the switches.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| ELECTRIC WINDOW CONTROLS ON DRIVER'S FRONT DOOR | See H044 ELECTRIC WINDOW CONTROLS ON FRONT DRIVER'S DOOR [→939004078] |
| ELECTRIC WINDOW SWITCH ON PASSENGER FRONT DOOR | See H050 ELECTRIC WINDOW SWITCH ON FRONT PASSENGER'S DOOR [→939004084] |
5. Keep the interior grab handle complete with switches.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000639.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the interior grab handle and the switches are not damaged.
- Connect the electrical connection for the switches.
- Place the grab handle in position and attach the retainers.
- Tighten the bolts.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| FRONT DOOR INTERIOR GRAB HANDLE | Bolt | M6x25 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
- Place the trim in position.
- Check that the switches are working properly.

## 7005M28 FRONT SIDE DOOR ELECTRICALLY OPERATED WINDOW MOTOR - R.R. WITH DOOR PANEL/SUPPORT FRAME REMOVED  [939011142]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7005 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT FRONT SIDE DOORS > 7005M FRONT ELECTRIC WINDOWS > 7005M28 FRONT SIDE DOOR ELECTRICALLY OPERATED WINDOW MOTOR - R.R. WITH DOOR PANEL/SUPPORT FRAME REMOVED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011142

##### Removing
1. Disconnect the electrical connection.
|  | Description | Component |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | DRIVER'S FRONT ELECTRIC WINDOW MOTOR | See N060 DRIVER'S FRONT ELECTRIC WINDOW MOTOR [→939004434] |
| 1 | PASSENGER SIDE FRONT ELECTRIC WINDOW MOTOR | See N061 RIGHT FRONT ELECTRIC WINDOW MOTOR [→939004437] |
2. Undo the bolts.
3. Remove the electric window motor releasing the splined shaft.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004138.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the electric window motor is not damaged.
> Note: The bushes in which the window motor bolts are engaged must be replaced.
1. Replace the bushes.
2. Position the window motor.
3. Tighten the fixing bolts.
4. Connect the electrical connection.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004139.jpg]

1. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| DRIVER'S FRONT DOOR LOCK GEARED MOTOR | See N050 DRIVER'S FRONT DOOR LOCK GEARED MOTOR [→939004415] |
| PASSENGER SIDE FRONT DOOR LOCK GEARED MOTOR | See N051 RIGHT FRONT DOOR LOCK GEARED MOTOR [→939004420] |
2. Release the seal (2a) for the rod (2b) from the frame.
3. Release the lock (3a) from the retainers (3b) for the support.
4. Keep the lock complete with rod.
5. If necessary, keep the lock support.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000642.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the lock is not damaged.
- If removed, place the lock support in position.
- Attach the lock to the support retainers.
- Fit the rod seal on the frame.
- Connect the electrical connection.
Op. 7005D24 FRONT DOOR WINDOW REAR GUIDE - R R [→939011130]

- Connect the electrical connection to the control unit.

## 7005R35 DOOR MIRROR REFLECTOR (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R  [939011151]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7005 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT FRONT SIDE DOORS > 7005R FRONT DOOR MIRRORS > 7005R35 DOOR MIRROR REFLECTOR (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011151

##### Removing
1. Tilt the reflector (1a) downwards until it is in the end of travel position, then insert a plastic blade (1b) between the reflector (1a) and the shell (1c) and release the reflector retainers (1d) from the inner circular support.
2. Disconnect the electrical connections for the reflector defroster.
3. Keep the reflector.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000644.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the reflector is not damaged.
- Connect the electrical connections for the reflector defroster.
- Position the reflector and attach the retainers.
- Check that the reflector is working.

Op. 7005R35 DOOR MIRROR REFLECTOR (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R [→939011151]
1. Undo the motor fixing bolts.
2. Move the motor (2a) slightly and disconnect the electrical connection (2b).
3. Keep the motor.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000645.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the motor is not damaged.
- Connect the electrical connection for the motor.

## PASSENGER COMPARTMENT REAR SIDE DOORS - GENERAL REMARKS  [939011200]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7010 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT REAR SIDE DOORS > PASSENGER COMPARTMENT REAR SIDE DOORS - GENERAL REMARKS
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011200

#### WARNINGS
Observe the following precautions when working on the rear doors assembly:
- When working on the hinges, take care not to damage the paintwork.
- Following an adjustment operation that required the hinges to be moved, the door must be removed and the hinge area repainted.
- Work carefully on the fittings and use the recommended tools correctly to prevent damage.

#### PRECAUTIONS
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.

## 7010A08 REAR SIDE DOOR INTERIOR SUPPORT FRAME - R.R. WITH DOOR PANEL REMOVED  [939011156]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7010 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT REAR SIDE DOORS > 7010A REAR SIDE DOOR ASSEMBLY > 7010A08 REAR SIDE DOOR INTERIOR SUPPORT FRAME - R.R. WITH DOOR PANEL REMOVED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011156

##### Removing
1. Open the access cover to the descending glass retainer.
2. Provisionally connect the window raise handle to control shaft (2a) or the window switch, then move the descending glass until glass drive retainer (2b) is in access position (as shown in the figure).
3. Disconnect retainer (2b) from the glass (3a).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001879.jpg]

1. Raise the window (1a) by hand until it is in the end of travel position, then secure it using a hook (1b).
2. Lift ring nut (2a) and disconnect the door electrical connection (2b).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| REAR JOINT-REAR DOOR LEFT | See D035 LEFT REAR DOOR COUPLING [→939003910] |
| REAR JOINT-REAR DOOR RIGHT | See D036 RIGHT REAR DOOR COUPLING [→939003915] |
3. Release the bulkhead coupling (3a) from the door frame opening (3b).
4. Push the electrical connection into the door frame as far as possible.
5. Undo the bolts fixing the support frame.
6. Loosen the lock fixing bolts.
7. Slightly move aside support frame (7a) from the front edge and release wiring retainers (7b).
8. Disconnect link (8a) form the door lock (8b), undoing retainers (8c) and (8d).
9. Recover the support frame.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001880.jpg]

- If necessary carry out
Op. 7205E39 STRIPPING REAR SIDE DOOR INTERIOR SUPPORT FRAME [→939011437]

##### Refitting
- Check that the support frame is not damaged.
- If necessary carry out
Op. 7205E39 STRIPPING REAR SIDE DOOR INTERIOR SUPPORT FRAME [→939011437]
1. Before positioning support frame (1a), check that the perimeter seal (1b) is undamaged and clean. Also check that the contact surface on the door frame is clean.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001881.jpg]

1. Position the support frame.
2. Connect link (2a) to door frame (2b), doing up retainers (2c) and (2d).
3. Fit connection (3a) into the door frame so that it emerges from opening (3b).
4. Secure the wiring to retainers (4a).
Check that wiring (4b) does not interfere with glass movement.
5. Tighten the lock fixing bolts.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| REAR DOOR OUTER TRIM | Bolt | M6x12 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
6. Place frame (6a) in position and secure it using the bolts (6b).
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| REAR DOOR OUTER TRIM | Bolt | M6x12 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
7. Fit the bulkhead coupling (7a) to the door frame opening (3b).
8. Connect electrical connection (8a) to the door and secure with ring nut (8b).
9. Remove the window retaining hook.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001882.jpg]

- Lower the window until it fits into the drive retainer.
- Position the rear channel descending section in its housing and secure with bolts.
- Close the access cover to the descending glass drive retainer.
- Move the window to check its operation.

## 7010A10 REAR SIDE DOOR - ADJUST STRIKER AND ADAPT SEAL IF NECESSARY  [939011158]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7010 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT REAR SIDE DOORS > 7010A REAR SIDE DOOR ASSEMBLY > 7010A10 REAR SIDE DOOR - ADJUST STRIKER AND ADAPT SEAL IF NECESSARY
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011158

> Block applies to: engines: 1.8, 1.9 JTS, 2.2 JTS, 3.2 JTS, 1.9 JTD 16V, 2.4 JTD 20V; all production periods
- Check that the front door is aligned, otherwise:
Op. 7010A10 REAR SIDE DOOR - ADJUST STRIKER AND ADAPT SEAL IF NECESSARY [→939011158]
- Open the rear door.
1. Check that the door seal is not damaged along its perimeter, otherwise replace.
2. Check that the upper reference (2a) coincides with the middle of angle (2b) and that joint (2c) coincides with the water drain groove (2d). Otherwise prise the seal out of the perimeter housing and refit, observing the markings.
3. If necessary, align bolts (3a) and move lock striker (3b) to the correct position using the slotted bolt holes. The arrows indicate the possible movements for the adjustment.
Tighten the bolts (3a) to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| REAR DOOR OUTER TRIM | Bolt | M6x12 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004140.jpg]

> Block applies to: engines: 1.8, 1.9 JTS, 2.2 JTS, 3.2 JTS, 1.9 JTD 16V, 2.4 JTD 20V; all production periods
- Check that the stiker and the door lock are correctly matched and that the alignment is correct with the door closed.

## 7010A14 REAR SIDE DOOR - ADJUST CENTERING AND MOULDING - INCLUDES ADAPTATION OF HINGES AND SEALS ON DOOR COMPARTMENT  [939011159]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7010 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT REAR SIDE DOORS > 7010A REAR SIDE DOOR ASSEMBLY > 7010A14 REAR SIDE DOOR - ADJUST CENTERING AND MOULDING - INCLUDES ADAPTATION OF HINGES AND SEALS ON DOOR COMPARTMENT
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011159

1. If the adjustment is carried out after having removed the door, tighten the hinge bolts to the recommended torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DOOR HALF HINGES | Bolt | M6x14 | 1.2 ÷ 1.8 |  |
Op. 7010B14 REAR SIDE DOOR LOCK STRIKER - R.R [→939011165]
- If necessary, tighten the bolts securing the hinges to the door and body.
2. Unscrew opening limiter bolts (2a) and close the door temporarily to return link (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001883.jpg]

- Initially adjust the door within the door compartment. To do this, the bolts securing the hinges to the body must be loosened.
Op. 7040L40 FRONT DOOR RUNNING BOARD (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011274]
Op. 7050A20 ONE-PIECE REAR SEAT CUSHION - R.R. [→939011325]
Op. 7040L44 REAR DOOR RUNNING BOARD (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011275]
Op. 7040H36 CENTRE PILLAR LOWER ELEMENT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R [→939011264]
1. Loosen the bolts (1a) securing hinges (1b) to the body.
2. Adjust the door position by means of the slotted bolt holes until it is correctly aligned in the compartment.
> Note: It is advisable to work on one hinge at a time. The arrows indicate the possible movements for the adjustment.
3. After adjustment, tighten the bolts to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DOOR HINGES TO BODYSHELL | Bolt | M8x30 | 2.9 ÷ 3.5 |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001884.jpg]

- Refit the components removed to access the hinge fixing bolts.
Op. 7040H36 CENTRE PILLAR LOWER ELEMENT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R [→939011264]
Op. 7040L44 REAR DOOR RUNNING BOARD (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011275]
Op. 7050A20 ONE-PIECE REAR SEAT CUSHION - R.R. [→939011325]
Op. 7040L40 FRONT DOOR RUNNING BOARD (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011274]
- If necessary, align the door with the wing and front door as follows.
- Open the door.
1. Loosen the bolts (1a) securing hinges (1b) to the body.
2. Adjust the door position by means of the slotted bolt holes until it is correctly aligned with the wing and front door.
> Note: It is advisable to work on one hinge at a time. The arrows indicate the possible movements for the adjustment.
3. After adjustment, tighten the bolts to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DOOR HINGES | Bolt | M8x30 | 2.9 ÷ 3.5 |  |
4. Remove link (4a) and tighten bolts (4b) to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DOOR CHECK STRAP | Bolt | M6x22 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001885.jpg]

- Refit and adjust the door striker.
Op. 7010B14 REAR SIDE DOOR LOCK STRIKER - R.R [→939011165]
- Touch up the paint in the hinge half area.
- Check that the stiker and the door lock are correctly matched and that the alignment is correct with the door closed.

## 7010A28 REAR SIDE DOOR OPENING LIMITER - R.R  [939011160]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7010 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT REAR SIDE DOORS > 7010A REAR SIDE DOOR ASSEMBLY > 7010A28 REAR SIDE DOOR OPENING LIMITER - R.R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011160

##### Removing
Op. 7010E62 REAR DOOR INNER REAR PANEL (ONE) LH OR RH - R R [→939011190]
Op. 7010E38 REAR SIDE DOOR INTERIOR HANDLE-R R [→939011187]

1. Unscrew the bolts on the body.
2. Unscrew the bolts on the door.
3. Release the seal.
4. Remove the limiter by extracting from the door frame.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000646.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the limiter and the seal are not damaged.
- Place the limiter in its seat.
- Tighten the nuts to the body to the recommended torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DOOR CHECK STRAP | Nut | M6 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
- Tighten the bolts to the body to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DOOR CHECK STRAP | Bolt | M6x22 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
- Fit the seal.
- Check the operation of the limiter.

## 7010A34 REAR SIDE DOOR COMPARTMENT SEAL - R.R  [939011161]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7010 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT REAR SIDE DOORS > 7010A REAR SIDE DOOR ASSEMBLY > 7010A34 REAR SIDE DOOR COMPARTMENT SEAL - R.R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011161

##### Removing
- Open the door.
1. Release the seal along the entire perimeter of the door housing.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000647.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the seal is not damaged.
1. Fit the seal (1e) in position ensuring that the reference (1a) is aligned with the centre of the corner (1b) and the join (1c) with the water drainage splining (1d).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000648.jpg]

- Check that the door shuts properly and is correctly aligned with the bodywork.

## 7010A46 LOWER REAR SIDE DOOR SEAL - R.R.  [939011162]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7010 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT REAR SIDE DOORS > 7010A REAR SIDE DOOR ASSEMBLY > 7010A46 LOWER REAR SIDE DOOR SEAL - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011162

##### Removing
- Open the door.
1. Release the retaining buttons using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
> Note: When refitting, replace the retaining buttons.
2. Keep the gasket.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000649.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the seal is not damaged.
- Place the seal in position and secure it using new retaining buttons.

## 7010B14 REAR SIDE DOOR LOCK STRIKER - R.R  [939011165]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7010 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT REAR SIDE DOORS > 7010B REAR DOOR MECHANICAL LOCKS > 7010B14 REAR SIDE DOOR LOCK STRIKER - R.R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011165

##### Removing
1. Open the door and undo the bolts.
2. Keep the striker.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000650.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the striker is not damaged.
1. Position the striker.
2. Screw in the bolts without tightening them.
3. Move the striker into the correct position, using the slotted openings for the bolts. The arrows indicate the possible movements for the adjustment.
4. Tighten the retaining bolts to the recommended torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| REAR DOOR OUTER TRIM | Bolt | M6x12 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000651.jpg]

- Check that the striker and the door lock are correctly matched and that the alignment is correctly with the door closed.

## 7010D15 REAR SIDE DOOR DESCENDING WINDOW GLASS (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R  [939011175]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7010 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT REAR SIDE DOORS > 7010D REAR SIDE DOOR WINDOW GLASSES > 7010D15 REAR SIDE DOOR DESCENDING WINDOW GLASS (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011175

##### Removing
Op. 7010E62 REAR DOOR INNER REAR PANEL (ONE) LH OR RH - R R [→939011190]
Op. 7010E38 REAR SIDE DOOR INTERIOR HANDLE-R R [→939011187]
For vehicles with manually operated windows
Op. 7010D38 REAR SIDE DOOR MANUAL WINDOW OPENING MECHANISM HANDLE/RING NUT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R [→939011183]
Op. 7010E10 REAR SIDE DOOR INNER PANEL - R.R. OR REPLACE [→939011186]
1. Open the access cover to the descending glass retainer.
2. Provisionally connect the window raising handle to the control shaft (2a) or the window switch, then move the descending glass until the glass drive retainer (2b) is in the access position (as shown in the figure).
3. Disconnect the retainer (2b) from the glass (3a).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001891.jpg]

1. Undo the bolts fixing the rear channel lowering section of the glass.
2. Move the lowering section of the channel to the side.
3. Pull the glass (3a) carefully in the direction of the arrow and release the pad (3b) from the channel.
4. Extract the glass carefully from the outside of the door frame.

[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001892.jpg]

- If necessary, carry out:
Op. 7010D12 REAR SIDE DOOR DESCENDING WINDOW GLASS FASTENING ELEMENT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011174]

##### Refitting
- Check the condition of the glass and the sliding channels.
- If removed,
Op. 7010D12 REAR SIDE DOOR DESCENDING WINDOW GLASS FASTENING ELEMENT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011174]
- Position the glass carefully in its housing and attach the pad in the channel.
- Lower the window until it fits into the drive retainer.
- Position the rear channel descending section in its housing and secure with bolts.
- Close the access cover to the descending glass drive retainer.
- Move the window to check its operation.
Op. 7010E10 REAR SIDE DOOR INNER PANEL - R.R. OR REPLACE [→939011186]
Op. 7010D38 REAR SIDE DOOR MANUAL WINDOW OPENING MECHANISM HANDLE/RING NUT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R [→939011183]
Op. 7010E38 REAR SIDE DOOR INTERIOR HANDLE-R R [→939011187]
Op. 7010E62 REAR DOOR INNER REAR PANEL (ONE) LH OR RH - R R [→939011190]
- Check that the dismantled parts are working.

- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7010 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT REAR SIDE DOORS > 7010D REAR SIDE DOOR WINDOW GLASSES > 7010D18 REAR SIDE DOOR DESCENDING WINDOW GLASS CHANNEL (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R

##### Removing
- Lower the window to the end of travel position.
1. Release the channel from the housing.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000654.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the channel is not damaged.
- Fit the channel in position.
- Move the window to check that it slides correct in the channel.

- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7010 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT REAR SIDE DOORS > 7010E REAR SIDE DOOR TRIM > 7010E10 REAR SIDE DOOR INNER PANEL - R.R. OR REPLACE
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011186

##### Removing
Op. 7010E62 REAR DOOR INNER REAR PANEL (ONE) LH OR RH - R R [→939011190]
Op. 7010E38 REAR SIDE DOOR INTERIOR HANDLE-R R [→939011187]
For vehicles with manual windows
Op. 7010D38 REAR SIDE DOOR MANUAL WINDOW OPENING MECHANISM HANDLE/RING NUT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R [→939011183]
1.Release the trim cover.
2. Undo the bolts.
3. Move the panel (3a) slightly releasing the inner retainers (3b) using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
4. Raise the panel to release its upper part, then disconnect electrical connection (4a) of tweeter (4b).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| LEFT REAR TWEETER SPEAKER | See P047 LEFT REAR TWEETER SPEAKER [→939004590] |
| RIGHT REAR TWEETER SPEAKER | See P048 RIGHT REAR TWEETER SPEAKER [→939004593] |
5. Releaase the retainers (5a) for the interior handle (5b).
6. Keep the inner panel.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000659.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the inner panel is not damaged.
- Attach the interior handle retainers.
- Connect the electrical connection for the tweeter.
- Place the panel in position and attach the inner retainers.
- Tighten the bolts.
- Fit the trim cover.

## 7010N20 REAR SIDE DOOR LOCK MOTOR/CATCH ASSEMBLY - R.R. WITH DOOR PANEL AND SUPPORT FRAME REMOVED  [939011192]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7010 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT REAR SIDE DOORS > 7010N REAR SIDE DOOR CENTRAL LOCKING SYSTEM > 7010N20 REAR SIDE DOOR LOCK MOTOR/CATCH ASSEMBLY - R.R. WITH DOOR PANEL AND SUPPORT FRAME REMOVED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011192

##### Removing
1. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| LEFT REAR DOOR LOCK GEARED MOTOR | See N055 LEFT REAR DOOR LOCK GEARED MOTOR [→939004425] |
| RIGHT REAR DOOR LOCK GEARED MOTOR | See N056 RIGHT REAR DOOR LOCK GEARED MOTOR [→939004427] |
2. Disconnect the lock (2a) from the support retainers (2b).
3. Release the rod (3b) seal (3a) from the frame.
4. Keep the lock complete with rod.
5. If necessary, keep the lock support (5a) after having removed the rivets (5b) using a drill.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000664.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the lock is not damaged.
- If removed, place the lock support in position and secure it using the rivets.
- Attach the lock to the support retainers.
- Fit the rod seal on the frame.
- Connect the electrical connection.

## 7010M20 REAR DOOR ELECTRIC WINDOW, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. WITH DOOR PANEL/SUPPORT FRAME REMOVED  [939011196]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7010 PASSENGER COMPARTMENT REAR SIDE DOORS > 7010M REAR SIDE DOOR ELECTRIC WINDOWS > 7010M20 REAR DOOR ELECTRIC WINDOW, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. WITH DOOR PANEL/SUPPORT FRAME REMOVED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011196

##### Removing
1. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| LEFT REAR DOOR LOCK GEARED MOTOR | See N055 LEFT REAR DOOR LOCK GEARED MOTOR [→939004425] |
| RIGHT REAR DOOR LOCK GEARED MOTOR | See N056 RIGHT REAR DOOR LOCK GEARED MOTOR [→939004427] |
2. Undo the bolts.
3. Remove the electric window motor releasing the splined shaft.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004143.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the electric window motor is not damaged.
> Note: The bush in which the window motor bolts are engaged must be replaced.
1. Replace the bushes.
2. Position the window motor.
3. Tighten the fixing bolts.
4. Connect the electrical connection.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004144.jpg]

## 7015A05 TAILGATE LOWER INTERIOR PANEL-R R  [939005348]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7015 TAILGATE/FLAP > 7015A TAILGATE ASSEMBLY > 7015A05 TAILGATE LOWER INTERIOR PANEL-R R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005348

##### Removing
- Open the tailgate.
Op. 7040G36 BULB HOLDER FOR LOAD CARRYING COMPARTMENT lighting on tailgate - R.R. [→939005460]
1. Remove the covers releasing the retainers.
2. Remove the rear light cluster access covers (2a), releasing the flexible retainers (2b) and then the rigid retainers (2c).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005244.jpg]

1. Undo the bolts.
2. Remove the inner panel (2a) releasing the inner retainers (2b) using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005245.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the inner panel is not damaged.
- Position the panel and engage the inner retainers.
- Tighten the bolts.
- Place the access covers on the rear light clusters and attach the rigid retainers and then the flexible retainers.
- Position the covers attaching the retainers.
Op. 7040G36 BULB HOLDER FOR LOAD CARRYING COMPARTMENT lighting on tailgate - R.R. [→939005460]
- Close the tailgate.

2. Undo the bolts.
3. Remove the inner panel (3a) releasing the inner retainers (3b) using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005246.jpg]

- Check that the inner panel is not damaged.

- Position the covers attaching the retainers.

Op. 7040G36 BULB HOLDER FOR LOAD CARRYING COMPARTMENT lighting on tailgate - R.R. [→939005460]

## 7015A10 TAILGATE - ADJUST STRIKER AND ADAPT SEAL IF NECESSARY  [939005350]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7015 TAILGATE/FLAP > 7015A TAILGATE ASSEMBLY > 7015A10 TAILGATE - ADJUST STRIKER AND ADAPT SEAL IF NECESSARY
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005350

- Open the tailgate.
1. Check that the self-adjusting buffers are working properly.
Replace them if necessary.
Op. 7015B12 TAILGATE SELF-ADJUSTING BUFFERS - R.R. [→939005444]
2. Check that the tailgate housing seal along the entire perimeter is working properly.
3. If necessary, adjust the position of the lock striker.
Op. 7015B18 TAILGATE LOCK STRIKER - R+R [→939005446]
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005247.jpg]

## 7015A14 TAILGATE - ADJUST CENTRING AND PROFILE - INCLUDES HINGE AND COMPARTMENT SEAL ADAPTATION  [939005351]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7015 TAILGATE/FLAP > 7015A TAILGATE ASSEMBLY > 7015A14 TAILGATE - ADJUST CENTRING AND PROFILE - INCLUDES HINGE AND COMPARTMENT SEAL ADAPTATION
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005351

- Open the tailgate.
1. Check that the self-adjusting buffers are working properly.
Replace them if necessary.
Op. 7015B12 TAILGATE SELF-ADJUSTING BUFFERS - R.R. [→939005444]
2. Check that the tailgate housing seal along the entire perimeter is working properly.
3. If necessary, adjust the position of the lock striker.
Op. 7015B18 TAILGATE LOCK STRIKER - R+R [→939005446]
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005248.jpg]

- If the adjustments indicated in the previous points are insufficient, adjust the position of the boot hinges as described below.
Support the boot lid appropriately when necessary.
1. Working from both sides of the vehicle, loosen the tailgate bolts and adjust the tailgate position using the slotted holes in the hinges.
> Note: The arrows indicate the possible movements.
2. After adjustment, tighten the bolts to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TAILGATE | Bolt | M6x16.5 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
3. If necessary, adjust the lock striker position as described below.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005249.jpg]

- If the adjustment of the fastening between the tailgate and the hinges is insufficient, proceed as follows.
Op. 7040H12 ROOF REAR TRIM - R.R. [→939005456]
Op. 7040G40 LOAD COMPARTMENT LIGHT BULB HOLDER - R R [→939005461]
Op. 7040B22 LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT/LOAD PLATFORM MOULDING - R+R [→939011251]
Op. 7040B04 SIDE MOULDING (ONE) LH OR RH FOR LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT - R.R. [→939005451]
Op. 7040B10 LOAD PLATFORM (LUGGAGE PLATFORM) TRIM - R+R [→939011248]
Op. 7040B18 LOAD COMPARTMENT LOAD PLATFORM SIDE TRIM ONE LH OR RH - R.R [→939011249]
Op. 7040B30 L/H OR R/H SIDE SUPPORT (ONE) FOR LOAD PLATFORM COVER BLIND - ARRANGE [→939005454]
Op. 7040H42 REAR PILLAR TRIM (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT- R.R [→939011266]
Op. 7040H68 TAI GATE HOUSING PILLAR TRIM (ONE), LH OR RH - R.R. [→939005457]
1. Working from both sides of the vehicle, loosen the bolts (1a) attaching the tailgate hinges to the body and adjust the hinge position using the slotted holes.
> Note: The arrows indicate the possible movements.
2. After adjustment, tighten the bolts to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TAILGATE HINGES | Bolt | M8x22 | 2.2 ÷ 2.6 |  |
- If necessary, adjust the lock striker position as described below.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005250.jpg]

- When the adjustment is completed, refit the components removed previously.

## 7015A24 TAILGATE HINGES - REPAIR OR REPLACE WITH TAILGATE REMOVED  [939005352]

- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005352

##### Removing
Op. 7040H12 ROOF REAR TRIM - R.R. [→939005456]
Op. 7040G40 LOAD COMPARTMENT LIGHT BULB HOLDER - R R [→939005461]
Op. 7040B22 LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT/LOAD PLATFORM MOULDING - R+R [→939011251]
Op. 7040B04 SIDE MOULDING (ONE) LH OR RH FOR LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT - R.R. [→939005451]
Op. 7040B10 LOAD PLATFORM (LUGGAGE PLATFORM) TRIM - R+R [→939011248]
Op. 7040B18 LOAD COMPARTMENT LOAD PLATFORM SIDE TRIM ONE LH OR RH - R.R [→939011249]
Op. 7040B30 L/H OR R/H SIDE SUPPORT (ONE) FOR LOAD PLATFORM COVER BLIND - ARRANGE [→939005454]
Op. 7040H42 REAR PILLAR TRIM (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT- R.R [→939011266]
Op. 7040H68 TAI GATE HOUSING PILLAR TRIM (ONE), LH OR RH - R.R. [→939005457]
1. Undo the boot lid hinge bolts.
2. Remove the hinges.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005251.jpg]

##### Refitting

- Restore the sealant between the hinges and the body, then position the hinges.

Op. 7015A14 TAILGATE - ADJUST CENTRING AND PROFILE - INCLUDES HINGE AND COMPARTMENT SEAL ADAPTATION [→939005351]
Op. 7040H68 TAI GATE HOUSING PILLAR TRIM (ONE), LH OR RH - R.R. [→939005457]
Op. 7040H42 REAR PILLAR TRIM (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT- R.R [→939011266]
Op. 7040B30 L/H OR R/H SIDE SUPPORT (ONE) FOR LOAD PLATFORM COVER BLIND - ARRANGE [→939005454]
Op. 7040B18 LOAD COMPARTMENT LOAD PLATFORM SIDE TRIM ONE LH OR RH - R.R [→939011249]
Op. 7040B10 LOAD PLATFORM (LUGGAGE PLATFORM) TRIM - R+R [→939011248]
Op. 7040B04 SIDE MOULDING (ONE) LH OR RH FOR LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT - R.R. [→939005451]
Op. 7040B22 LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT/LOAD PLATFORM MOULDING - R+R [→939011251]
Op. 7040G40 LOAD COMPARTMENT LIGHT BULB HOLDER - R R [→939005461]
Op. 7040H12 ROOF REAR TRIM - R.R. [→939005456]
- After refitting the tailgate, carry out the adjustment.

## 7015A28 L/H OR R/H TAILGATE TELESCOPIC STRUT (ONE) - R+R  [939005440]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7015 TAILGATE/FLAP > 7015A TAILGATE ASSEMBLY > 7015A28 L/H OR R/H TAILGATE TELESCOPIC STRUT (ONE) - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005440

##### Removing
1. Open the tailgate and support it.
2. Using a screwdriver, release the retaining springs from both ends of the telescopic strut (without removing them).
3. Remove the telescopic strut.
4. Undo the ball pins, if necessary.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005252.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the telescopic strut is not damaged.
- If removed, retighten the ball pins.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TELESCOPIC STRUT BALL JOINT | - | - | 2.2 ÷ 2.7 |  |
- Refit the telescopic strut, press fitting the ends in the ball pins.
- Fit the retaining springs.
- Check that the telescopic strut is working and that the tailgate is correctly supported.

## 7015A30 TAILGATE COMPARTMENT SEAL - R+R  [939005441]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7015 TAILGATE/FLAP > 7015A TAILGATE ASSEMBLY > 7015A30 TAILGATE COMPARTMENT SEAL - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005441

##### Removing
- Open the tailgate.
1. Release the seal and remove it.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005253.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the seal is intact and there are no signs of damage.
1. Place the seal (1a) in position so that the join (1b) is in the centre of the lower side.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005254.jpg]

- Check that the contact between the seal and the tailgate is correct.

## 7015B TAILGATE CLOSING/OPENING DEVICES  [939005442]

## 7015B10 SINGLE TAILGATE LOCK - R+R OR REPAIR WITH PANEL/PROTECTION REMOVED  [939005443]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7015 TAILGATE/FLAP > 7015B TAILGATE CLOSING/OPENING DEVICES > 7015B10 SINGLE TAILGATE LOCK - R+R OR REPAIR WITH PANEL/PROTECTION REMOVED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005443

##### Removing
1. Remove the lock protection (1a) releasing the inner retainers (1b) using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005255.jpg]

1. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| BOOT LOCK GEARED MOTOR | See N057 BOOT LOCK GEARED MOTOR [→939004429] |
2. Undo the bolts.
3. Keep the lock and motor and bridge.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005256.jpg]

- If necessary, remove the motor.
Op. 7015B70 TAILGATE LOCK MOTOR - R.R AND CHECK LOCK SUPPLY LINE [→939005448]

##### Refitting
- Check that the lock and the motor and bridge are not damaged.
- If necessary, refit the motor.
Op. 7015B70 TAILGATE LOCK MOTOR - R.R AND CHECK LOCK SUPPLY LINE [→939005448]
- Position the lock and motor and bridge.
- Tighten the bolts to the recommended torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TAILGATE LOCK | Bolt | M6x16 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
- Connect the electrical connection.
- Position the lock protection and attach the inner retainers.

## 7015B12 TAILGATE SELF-ADJUSTING BUFFERS - R.R.  [939005444]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7015 TAILGATE/FLAP > 7015B TAILGATE CLOSING/OPENING DEVICES > 7015B12 TAILGATE SELF-ADJUSTING BUFFERS - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005444

##### Removing
- Open the tailgate.
1. Undo the end of travel buffers ring nut.
2. Remove the buffers from the housings in the tailgate using a screwdriver for leverage and raising the support plane.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/946000685.jpg]

> Note: The buffers removed cannot be reused.

##### Refitting
- Check that the new buffers are not damaged.
1. Fit the new buffers (1a) in place, without rotating the ring nut (1b) in relation to the base (1c).
Place the mounting base (1c) in contact with the panel.
> Note: There is only one mounting fitting position.
- Close the tailgate twice, checking that it closes correctly and completely.
> Note: During this stage the end of travel buffers adjust themselves by means of a movement inside their moving casing.
- Open the tailgate again.
2. Rotate the ring nut (1c) clockwise through around 340° until it is aligned with the references (2a and 2b) on the ring nut and on the base.
- Close the tailgate again and check that it shuts properly and the profile is correct.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/946000686.jpg]

## 7015B18 TAILGATE LOCK STRIKER - R+R  [939005446]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7015 TAILGATE/FLAP > 7015B TAILGATE CLOSING/OPENING DEVICES > 7015B18 TAILGATE LOCK STRIKER - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005446

##### Removing
Op. 7040B22 LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT/LOAD PLATFORM MOULDING - R+R [→939011251]
1. Undo the bolts.
2. Remove the striker.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005257.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the striker is not damaged.
1. Place the striker in position and screw in the bolts without tightening them.
2. Adjust the position of the striker; the arrows show the possible movements.
> Note: The adjustment of the striker can also be carried out with the moulding fitted.
3. Tighten the bolts.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TAILGATE STRIKER | Bolt | M6x16.5 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005258.jpg]

Op. 7040B22 LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT/LOAD PLATFORM MOULDING - R+R [→939011251]

## 7015B45 EMERGENCY CONTROL FOR OPENING TAILGATE - R.R.  [939005447]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7015 TAILGATE/FLAP > 7015B TAILGATE CLOSING/OPENING DEVICES > 7015B45 EMERGENCY CONTROL FOR OPENING TAILGATE - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005447

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Station Wagon
##### Removing
Op. 7040B22 LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT/LOAD PLATFORM MOULDING - R+R [→939011251]
- Work on the left side.
Op. 7040G40 LOAD COMPARTMENT LIGHT BULB HOLDER - R R [→939005461]
Op. 7040B04 SIDE MOULDING (ONE) LH OR RH FOR LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT - R.R. [→939005451]
Op. 7040B10 LOAD PLATFORM (LUGGAGE PLATFORM) TRIM - R+R [→939011248]
Op. 7040B18 LOAD COMPARTMENT LOAD PLATFORM SIDE TRIM ONE LH OR RH - R.R [→939011249]
- Raise the rear seat left cushion.
1. Loosen the bolts fixing the tailgate lock striker.
2. Move the striker (2a) and disconnect the emergency opening rod (2b) releasing the retainers (2c).
3. Partly detach the adhesive protection to release the emergency control.
4. Disconnect the emergency control from the intermediate retainers.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005476.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Station Wagon
1. working in the area of the rear seat left cushion, disconnect emergency control (1a) from retainer (1b) and recover.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005477.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Station Wagon
##### Refitting
- Check that the emergency control is not damaged.
- Place the emergency cover in position.
- Working in area of the rear seat left cushion, connect the emergency control to the retainer.
- Connect the emergency control to the intermediate retainers.
- Position and bond the adhesive protection securing the emergency control.
- Connect the emergency control to the tailgate lock striker retainers.
- Position the tailgate lock striker and screw in the fixing bolts without tightening them.
Op. 7040B18 LOAD COMPARTMENT LOAD PLATFORM SIDE TRIM ONE LH OR RH - R.R [→939011249]
Op. 7040B10 LOAD PLATFORM (LUGGAGE PLATFORM) TRIM - R+R [→939011248]
Op. 7040B04 SIDE MOULDING (ONE) LH OR RH FOR LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT - R.R. [→939005451]
Op. 7040G40 LOAD COMPARTMENT LIGHT BULB HOLDER - R R [→939005461]
Op. 7040B22 LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT/LOAD PLATFORM MOULDING - R+R [→939011251]
Op. 7015A10 TAILGATE - ADJUST STRIKER AND ADAPT SEAL IF NECESSARY [→939005350]
- Check the operation of the tailgate emergency opening control
- Lower the rear seat left cushion.

## 7015B70 TAILGATE LOCK MOTOR - R.R AND CHECK LOCK SUPPLY LINE  [939005448]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7015 TAILGATE/FLAP > 7015B TAILGATE CLOSING/OPENING DEVICES > 7015B70 TAILGATE LOCK MOTOR - R.R AND CHECK LOCK SUPPLY LINE
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005448

##### Removing
Op. 7040G36 BULB HOLDER FOR LOAD CARRYING COMPARTMENT lighting on tailgate - R.R. [→939005460]
Op. 7015A05 TAILGATE LOWER INTERIOR PANEL-R R [→939005348]
Op. 7015B10 SINGLE TAILGATE LOCK - R+R OR REPAIR WITH PANEL/PROTECTION REMOVED [→939005443]
1. Undo the bolts.
2. Remove the lock (2a) from the motor (2b).
3. If necessary, undo the bolts (3a), then disconnect the electrical connection (3b) and separate the bridge (3c) from the motor (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005259.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the motor and the bridge are not damaged.
- If removed, position the bridge, then connect the electrical connection and tighten the bolts.
- Position the motor on the lock.
- Tighten the bolts.
Op. 7015B10 SINGLE TAILGATE LOCK - R+R OR REPAIR WITH PANEL/PROTECTION REMOVED [→939005443]
Op. 7015A05 TAILGATE LOWER INTERIOR PANEL-R R [→939005348]
Op. 7040G36 BULB HOLDER FOR LOAD CARRYING COMPARTMENT lighting on tailgate - R.R. [→939005460]

## GENERAL REMARKS - COMPARTMENT COVERS  [939011227]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7025 COMPARTMENT COVERS > GENERAL REMARKS - COMPARTMENT COVERS
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011227

#### WARNINGS
When working on the compartment covers, follow these precautions:
- When working on the hinges, take care not to damage the paintwork.
- After an adjustment to a door that has involved moving the hinges, the paintwork in the hinge area must be renewed.
- Work carefully on the fittings and use the recommended tools correctly to prevent damage.

#### PRECAUTIONS
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.
Always move the covers with the help of an assistant.

## 7025A10 BONNET LID - ALIGNMENT  [939004932]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7025 COMPARTMENT COVERS > 7025A BONNET LID > 7025A10 BONNET LID - ALIGNMENT
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004932

- Open the bonnet.
1. Check that the self-adjusting buffers are working properly.
Replace them if necessary.
Op. 7025A15 BONNET LID SELF-ADJUSTING BUFFERS - R.R. [→939004933]
2. If necessary, adjust the position of the lock.
Op. 7025A34 BONNET LID LOCK (ONE) - R+R OR REPAIR [→939011207]
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/946000510.jpg]

- If the adjustments indicated in the previous points are insufficient, adjust the position of the bonnet hinges as described below.
1. Working from both sides of the vehicle, loosen the bonnet lid bolts and adjust the lid position using the slotted holes in the hinges.
> Note: The arrows indicate the possible movements.
After adjustment, tighten the bolts to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| BONNET | Bolt | M8x20 | 2.2 ÷ 2.6 |  |
2. If necessary, working from both sides of the vehicle, loosen the lid bolts and adjust the lid position using the slotted holes in the hinges.
> Note: The arrows indicate the possible movements.
After adjustment, tighten the bolts to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| BONNET HINGES | Bolt | M8x25 | 2.2 ÷ 2.6 |  |
3. If necessary. Adjust the hinge mounts as follows.
Op. 7210E22 R.R. WINDSCREEN BASE TRIM [→939011397]
- Working from both sides of the vehicle, bolts attaching the hinges to the body and adjust the lid position using the slotted holes in the hinges.
> Note: The arrows indicate the possible movements.
After adjustment, tighten the bolts to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| BONNET HINGE BRACKET | Bolt | M6 | 0.8 ÷ 1.0 |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001897.jpg]

- If necessary, adjust the position of the lock.
Op. 7025A34 BONNET LID LOCK (ONE) - R+R OR REPAIR [→939011207]

1. Fit the new buffers (1a) in place, without rotating the ring nut (1b) in relation to the base (1c).
Place the mounting base (1c) in contact with the panel.
> Note: There is only one mounting fitting position.
- Close the bonnet twice, checking that it closes correctly and completely.
> Note: During this stage the end of travel buffers adjust themselves by means of a movement inside their moving casing.
- Open the bonnet again.
2. Rotate the ring nut (1c) clockwise through around 340° until it is aligned with the references (2a and 2b) on the ring nut and on the base.
- Close the bonnet again and check that it shuts properly and the profile is correct.

## 7025A18 L/H OR R/H BONNET LID HINGE (ONE) - R+R WITH LID REMOVED  [939011205]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7025 COMPARTMENT COVERS > 7025A BONNET LID > 7025A18 L/H OR R/H BONNET LID HINGE (ONE) - R+R WITH LID REMOVED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011205

##### Removing
For the left and right sides
Op. 5050B34 WINDSCREEN WIPER ARM/S COMPLETE WITH BLADE - R+R [→939010947]
Op. 7025A64 ENGINE COMPARTMENT GASKET - R.R. [→939011203]
For the left hinge, proceed as follows:
Op. 7210E22 R.R. WINDSCREEN BASE TRIM [→939011397]
1. Undo the bolts.
2. Remove the left hinge.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001898.jpg]

For the right hinge, proceed as follows:
1. Release the retaining buttons using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
2. Remove the right half trim (2a) releasing the internal retainers (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001899.jpg]

1. Undo the bolts.
2. Remove the right hinge.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001900.jpg]

##### Refitting
For the left hinge, proceed as follows:
- Check that the left hinge is not damaged.
- Position the hinge.
- Screw in the bolts without tightening them.
- After refitting the bonnet lid, adjust the position.
Op. 7025A10 BONNET LID - ALIGNMENT [→939004932]
Op. 7210E22 R.R. WINDSCREEN BASE TRIM [→939011397]
Op. 7025A64 ENGINE COMPARTMENT GASKET - R.R. [→939011203]
Op. 5050B34 WINDSCREEN WIPER ARM/S COMPLETE WITH BLADE - R+R [→939010947]
For the right hinge, proceed as follows:
- Check that the right hinge is not damaged.
- Position the hinge.
- Screw in the bolts without tightening them.
- After refitting the bonnet lid, adjust the position.
Op. 7025A10 BONNET LID - ALIGNMENT [→939004932]
- Place the right trim in position and attach the inner retainers.
- Do up the retaining studs.
Op. 7025A64 ENGINE COMPARTMENT GASKET - R.R. [→939011203]

## 7025A24 BONNET LID OPENING LEVER - R+R  [939011211]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7025 COMPARTMENT COVERS > 7025A BONNET LID > 7025A24 BONNET LID OPENING LEVER - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011211

##### Removing
1. Working under the dashboard in the area of the driver's side panel, unscrew the bolts securing the bonnet lid opening lever.
2. Move the lever slightly
3. Disconnect the control cable (3a) from lever retainers (3b).
4. Keep the lever.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004146.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Connect the control cable to the lever retainers.
- Position the lever and tighten the bolts.
- Check the operation of the bonnet lid opening mechanism.

## 7025A28 BONNET LID OPENING CABLE - R R  [939011212]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7025 COMPARTMENT COVERS > 7025A BONNET LID > 7025A28 BONNET LID OPENING CABLE - R R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011212

##### Removing
Op. 7025A24 BONNET LID OPENING LEVER - R+R [→939011211]
Op. 7055B54 UNDER ENGINE PROTECTION/GUARD - R.R. [→939011369]
Op. 7210E10 R.R. FRONT BUMPER [→939011399]
Op. 7025A34 BONNET LID LOCK (ONE) - R+R OR REPAIR [→939011207]
Op. 5530B10 BATTERY - R+R [→939011011]
Op. 5530B52 BATTERY SUPPORT/DRIP TRAY - R.R [→939011009]
1. Release retainers (1a) of transmission cable (1b).
2. Fit a sensor to the end (2a) of the transmission cable (1b), then extract the transmission cable (2b), leaving the sensor in its place (to be used for reinserting the cable when refitting).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004147.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the control cable is not damaged.
- Use the probe to reinsert the transmission cable in its seat.
> Note: Avoid bending the bowden cable or kinking it so that it could become deformed.
- Secure the bowden cable correctly to its retainers.
Op. 5530B52 BATTERY SUPPORT/DRIP TRAY - R.R [→939011009]

## 7025A34 BONNET LID LOCK (ONE) - R+R OR REPAIR  [939011207]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7025 COMPARTMENT COVERS > 7025A BONNET LID > 7025A34 BONNET LID LOCK (ONE) - R+R OR REPAIR
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011207

##### Removing
- Open the bonnet.
1. Undo the upper bolts fixing the front bumper.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000666.jpg]

1. Undo the lock retaining nuts.
2. Move the catch (2a) slightly aside and disconnect retainers (2b) and (2c) from the operating cable (2d).
3. Recover catch (2a) by moving the upper edge (3a) of the bumper slightly.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000667.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the lock is not damaged.
- Slightly move the upper edge of the bumper and position the lock.
- Connect the control cable to the lock retainers.
- Screw in the lock nuts without tightening them.
- Tighten the upper bolts fixing the front bumper.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| FRONT BUMPER | Bolt | M6x22 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
1. Adjust the position of the lock.
> Note: The arrows indicate the possible movements.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000668.jpg]

- Tighten the lock nuts to the recommended torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| BONNET LID LOCK | Nut | M6 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
- Check that the bonnet opens and closes properly.

2. Keep the weather strip.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001902.jpg]

- Check that the weather strip is not damaged.
- Position the weather strip and do up the retaining studs.

## 7025A64 ENGINE COMPARTMENT GASKET - R.R.  [939011203]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7025 COMPARTMENT COVERS > 7025A BONNET LID > 7025A64 ENGINE COMPARTMENT GASKET - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011203

##### Removing
- Open the bonnet.
1. Release the seal starting at one end.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000665.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the seal is intact and there are no signs of damage.
- Position the seal and fit it in its housing starting at one end.
- Check that the contact between the seal and the bonnet is correct.

## 7025A66 L/H OR R/H BONNET LID SUPPORT DAMPER (ONE) - R+R  [939011209]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7025 COMPARTMENT COVERS > 7025A BONNET LID > 7025A66 L/H OR R/H BONNET LID SUPPORT DAMPER (ONE) - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011209

##### Removing
- Open the bonnet and support it appropriately.
1. Using a screwdriver, release the retaining springs from both ends of the telescopic strut (without removing them).
2. Remove the telescopic strut.
3. Undo the ball pins, if necessary.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001903.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the telescopic strut is not damaged.
- If removed, retighten the ball pins.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TELESCOPIC STRUT BALL JOINT |  |  | 2.2 ÷ 2.6 |  |
- Refit the telescopic strut, press fitting the ends in the ball pins.
- Fit the retaining springs.
- Check the operation of the telescopic strut and ensure the boot lid is correctly supported.
