## 7025B10 BOOT LID - SQUARE  [939011214]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7025 COMPARTMENT COVERS > 7025B BOOT LID > 7025B10 BOOT LID - SQUARE
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011214

- Open the boot lid.
1. Check that the boot lid seal is in place around the entire perimeter.
2. If necessary, adjust the buffers.
3. If necessary, adjust the striker position as described below.
4. Loosen the bolts fixing the lock striker.
5. Adjust the position of the lock striker using the slotted holes. The arrows indicate the possible movements for the adjustment.
6. Tighten the striker fixing bolts.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| BOOT LID LOCK | Bolt | M6x16 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001905.jpg]

- If the adjustments indicated in the previous points are insufficient, adjust the position of the boot hinges as described below.
- Remove both lift devices.
Op. 7025B26 L/H OR R/H BOOT LID LIFT DEVICE (ONE) - R+R [→939011217]
1. Support the boot lid.
2. Working from both sides of the vehicle, loosen the boot lid bolts and adjust the boot lid position using the slotted holes in the hinges.
> Note: The arrows indicate the possible movements.
3. After adjustment, tighten the bolts to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| BOOT LID LOCK | Bolt | M6x16 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
- If necessary, adjust the lock striker position as described below.
- If the adjustment of the fastening between boot lid and hinges is insufficient, proceed as follows.
4. Working from both sides of the vehicle, loosen the bolts attaching the boot lid hinges to the body and adjust the hinge position using the slotted holes.
> Note: The arrows indicate the possible movements.
5. After adjustment, tighten the bolts to the specified torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| BOOT LID HINGES | Bolt | M6x16.5 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
- If necessary, adjust the lock striker position as described below.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001906.jpg]

- Refit the lift devices.
Op. 7025B26 L/H OR R/H BOOT LID LIFT DEVICE (ONE) - R+R [→939011217]

## 7025B34 BOOT LID EMERGENCY OPENING CONTROL - R.R.  [939005556]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7025 COMPARTMENT COVERS > 7025B BOOT LID > 7025B34 BOOT LID EMERGENCY OPENING CONTROL - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005556

##### Removing
Op. 7040B20 BACK TRIM FOR LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT/LOAD PLATFORM - ARRANGE [→939011250]
Op. 7040B10 LOAD PLATFORM (LUGGAGE PLATFORM) TRIM - R+R [→939011248]
Op. 7025B66 BOOT COMPARTMENT SEAL - R+R [→939011219]
Op. 7040B22 LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT/LOAD PLATFORM MOULDING - R+R [→939011251]
Op. 7040B18 LOAD COMPARTMENT LOAD PLATFORM SIDE TRIM ONE LH OR RH - R.R [→939011249]
- Raise the rear seat left cushion.
- Or, alternatively
Op. 7050A20 ONE-PIECE REAR SEAT CUSHION - R.R. [→939011325]
1. Undo the boot lid lock striker bolts.
2. Move the striker (2a) and disconnect the emergency opening rod (2b) releasing the retainers (2c).
3. Partly detach the adhesive protection to release the emergency control.
4. Disconnect the emergency control from the intermediate retainers.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005478.jpg]

1. working in the area of the rear seat left cushion, disconnect emergency control (1a) from retainer (1b) and recover.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005479.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the emergency control is not damaged.
- Place the emergency cover in position.
- Working in area of the rear seat left cushion, connect the emergency control to the retainer.
- Connect the emergency control to the intermediate retainers.
- Position and bond the adhesive protection securing the emergency control.
- Connect the emergency control to the boot lock striker retainers.
- Position the boot lock striker and screw in the fixing bolts without tightening them.
Op. 7040B18 LOAD COMPARTMENT LOAD PLATFORM SIDE TRIM ONE LH OR RH - R.R [→939011249]
Op. 7040B22 LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT/LOAD PLATFORM MOULDING - R+R [→939011251]
Op. 7025B66 BOOT COMPARTMENT SEAL - R+R [→939011219]
Op. 7040B10 LOAD PLATFORM (LUGGAGE PLATFORM) TRIM - R+R [→939011248]
Op. 7040B20 BACK TRIM FOR LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT/LOAD PLATFORM - ARRANGE [→939011250]
Op. 7025B10 BOOT LID - SQUARE [→939011214]
- Check the operation of the boot emergency opening control
- Lower the rear seat left cushion.
- Or, alternatively
Op. 7050A20 ONE-PIECE REAR SEAT CUSHION - R.R. [→939011325]

## 7025B38 BOOT LID LOCK - R+R  [939011220]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7025 COMPARTMENT COVERS > 7025B BOOT LID > 7025B38 BOOT LID LOCK - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011220

##### Removing
Op. 7025B41 BOOT LID LOCK [→939004820]

1. Release the retainer for the electrical connection from the boot lid.
2. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| BOOT LOCK GEARED MOTOR | See N057 BOOT LOCK GEARED MOTOR [→939004429] |
3. Undo the bolts.
4. Remove the lock and motor and wiring.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001911.jpg]

- If necessary,
Op. 7025B70 BOOT LOCK MOTOR - R R AND CHECK LOCK SUPPLY LINE [→939011221]

##### Refitting
- Check that the lock and the motor are not damaged.
- If removed,
Op. 7025B70 BOOT LOCK MOTOR - R R AND CHECK LOCK SUPPLY LINE [→939011221]
- Position the lock and motor and wiring.
- Tighten the bolts to the recommended torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| BOOT LID LOCK | Bolt | M6x16 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
- Connect the electrical connection.
- Attach the retainer for the electrical connection to the boot lid.
Op. 7025B41 BOOT LID LOCK [→939004820]
- Check that the lock is working.

## 7025B70 BOOT LOCK MOTOR - R R AND CHECK LOCK SUPPLY LINE  [939011221]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7025 COMPARTMENT COVERS > 7025B BOOT LID > 7025B70 BOOT LOCK MOTOR - R R AND CHECK LOCK SUPPLY LINE
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)

##### Removing
Op. 7025B38 BOOT LID LOCK - R+R [→939011220]
1. Undo the bolts.
2. Remove the lock (2a) from the motor (2b).
3. If necessary, undo the bolts (3a), then disconnect the electrical connection (3b) and separate the wiring (3c) from the motor (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000673.jpg]

##### Refitting

## 7025C10 FUEL FILLER ACCESS FLAP - R+R  [939011224]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7025 COMPARTMENT COVERS > 7025C VARIOUS FLAPS (EXTERIOR) > 7025C10 FUEL FILLER ACCESS FLAP - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011224

##### Removing
- Position the vehicle on a lift.
- Raise the vehicle.
1. Undo the rear bolts fixing the right rear wheel arch liner.
2. Undo the nuts fixing the wheel arch.
3. Move the rear section of the wheel arch slightly after having released it from the housing.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001912.jpg]

1. Open the fuel filler cap access flap.
2. Working via the wheel arch housing, undo the inner flap fixing nuts.
3. Disconnect the retainer.
4. Remove the flap.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001913.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the flap is not damaged.
- Position the flap.
- Connect the retainer.
- Working via the wheel arch housing, screw in the inner flap fixing nuts without tightening them.
- Adjust the position of the flap in relation to the bodywork, then tighten the nuts.
- Place the wheel arch in position.
- Tighten the nuts and the bolts.
- Remove the vehicle from the lift.

## 7025C32 FUEL FILLER FLAP CATCH CONTROL - R+R  [939011225]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7025 COMPARTMENT COVERS > 7025C VARIOUS FLAPS (EXTERIOR) > 7025C32 FUEL FILLER FLAP CATCH CONTROL - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011225

##### Removing
- Open the boot lid.
1. Undo the nut fixing the access cover for the right outside light.
2. Remove the access cover (2a) for the light, releasing the retainer (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001914.jpg]

- Open the fuel filler cap access flap.
1. Undo the motor fixing bolts.
2. Undo the fuel filler cap.
3. Gently extract the motor support.
- Retighten the fuel filler cap.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001915.jpg]

1. Disconnect the emergency control cord (1a) from the motor retainer (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001916.jpg]

1. Working in the luggage compartment, release the emergency control cord (1a) from the opening (1b) in the lining.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001917.jpg]

##### Refitting
1. Check that the emergency control cord is not damaged. Working in the luggage compartment, pass the emergency control cord (1a) through the opening (1b) in the lining, then make the end come out in the fuel filler using a hand in the compartment.(1c).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001918.jpg]

- Connect the emergency control cord to the motor retainer.
- Undo the fuel filler cap.
- Place the support and motor in position and secure them using the bolts.
- Retighten the fuel filler cap.
- Close the fuel filler cap access flap.
- Position the right outer light cover and attach the retainer.
- Tighten the nut.
- Close the boot lid.

## GENERAL REMARKS - SUN ROOF AND HOOD  [939011393]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7030 SUN ROOF/GLASS PANEL > GENERAL REMARKS - SUN ROOF AND HOOD
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011393

#### WARNINGS
Work carefully on the fittings and use the recommended tools correctly to prevent damage.
Take care to avoid any damage to the body paintwork.

#### PRECAUTIONS
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: therefore during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will thereby remain unlocked and free to turn.

## 7030B10 SLIDING ROOF (ASSEMBLY) - R+R WITH ROOF PANEL REMOVED  [939004822]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7030 SUN ROOF/GLASS PANEL > 7030B SLIDING ROOF > 7030B10 SLIDING ROOF (ASSEMBLY) - R+R WITH ROOF PANEL REMOVED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004822

##### Removing
- If possible, close the sliding sun roof.
1. Disconnect the electrical connection for the sun roof control unit.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| SUN ROOF COUPLING | See D045 SUN ROOF COUPLING [→939003927] |
2. Disconnect the water drainage pipes on both sides of the vehicle.
3. Undo the nuts (3a) and the bolts (3b) for the side brackets.
4. Remove the brackets.
5. Undo the sun roof retaining nuts.
6. Undo the sun roof retaining bolts.
7. Remove the sun roof.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004787.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the sun roof components are not damaged.
- Place the sun roof in position.
- Tighten the nuts and the bolts securing the sun roof.
- Place the side brackets in position and secure them using the nuts and bolts.
- Connect the water drainage pipes.
- Connect the electrical connection for the motor.
- If necessary,
Op. 7030B17 SUN-ROOF GLASS - ALIGNMENT [→939004825]
- Check that the sun roof is working properly.

## 7030B17 SUN-ROOF GLASS - ALIGNMENT  [939004825]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7030 SUN ROOF/GLASS PANEL > 7030B SLIDING ROOF > 7030B17 SUN-ROOF GLASS - ALIGNMENT
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004825

1. Place the sun roof in the closed position and open the blind.
2. Working on both sides, release the protection.
3. Loosen the bolts.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004788.jpg]

1. Fit a 4 mm shim (1a) between the rear of the transparent cover and the wall of the roof housing.
2. Tighten the rear bolts (2b), on both the left and the right.
3. Adjust the right front section (3a) of the transparent cover (2c) to 1 mm lower than the outside roof profile (as illustrated in the diagram), then tighten the right front bolt (2a).
4. Loosen the bolts (2b), on both the left and the right.
5. Adjust the right rear section (5a) of the transparent cover (2c) to 1 mm higher than the outside roof profile (as illustrated in the diagram), then tighten the right bolt (2b).
6. Repeat points 3 and 5 for the left side of the transparent cover.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004789.jpg]

- Working on both sides, fit the protection.
- Check that the sun roof is working properly in its various positions.

## 7030B18 SLIDING ROOF GLASS PANEL - R+R  [939004826]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7030 SUN ROOF/GLASS PANEL > 7030B SLIDING ROOF > 7030B18 SLIDING ROOF GLASS PANEL - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004826

##### Removing
1. Open the sun blind.
2. Working on both sides, release the protection.
3. Undo the bolts.
4. Remove the transparent panel.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004790.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the glass is not damaged.
- Reposition the transparent panel.
- Screw in the bolts without tightening them.
Op. 7030B17 SUN-ROOF GLASS - ALIGNMENT [→939004825]

## 7030B22 GLASS SEAL - R+R WITH GLASS REMOVED  [939004827]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7030 SUN ROOF/GLASS PANEL > 7030B SLIDING ROOF > 7030B22 GLASS SEAL - R+R WITH GLASS REMOVED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004827

##### Removing
1. Release the gasket.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004791.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the seal is not damaged.
- Fit the seal.

## 7030B28 SLIDING ROOF BLIND - R+R  [939004828]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7030 SUN ROOF/GLASS PANEL > 7030B SLIDING ROOF > 7030B28 SLIDING ROOF BLIND - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004828

##### Removing
Op. 7030B18 SLIDING ROOF GLASS PANEL - R+R [→939004826]
1. Disconnect the rods on both sides.
2. Move the drainage channel backwards.
3. Using the special tool, gently press the blind pads inwards and release them from the guide.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823014000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
4. Extract the blind releasing the pads on the opposite side.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004792.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the blind is not damaged.
- Place the blind pads on one side in position then, using the special tool, press the pads on the other side gently inwards and fit them in position.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823014000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
- Move the drainage channel forwards and connect the rods.
Op. 7030B18 SLIDING ROOF GLASS PANEL - R+R [→939004826]
- Check that the blind and the sun roof are working properly.

## 7030B34 SLIDING ROOF COMPARTMENT DECORATIVE SEAL - R+R  [939004829]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7030 SUN ROOF/GLASS PANEL > 7030B SLIDING ROOF > 7030B34 SLIDING ROOF COMPARTMENT DECORATIVE SEAL - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004829

##### Removing
1. Open the sun blind.
2. Release the seal (2a) starting from the join (2b) at the rear.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004793.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the seal is not damaged.
- Fit the seal, starting at the join which should be in the centre at the back.
- Check that the sun blind and the sunroof are working properly.

## 7030B38 SLIDING ROOF DRAINAGE PIPES (WATER DRAIN) - CHECK  [939004830]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7030 SUN ROOF/GLASS PANEL > 7030B SLIDING ROOF > 7030B38 SLIDING ROOF DRAINAGE PIPES (WATER DRAIN) - CHECK
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004830

- Position the vehicle on a lift.
Op. 7030B18 SLIDING ROOF GLASS PANEL - R+R [→939004826]
- Operate the sun roof so that the guides are in the fully open position.
- Remove any foreign bodies (leaves, etc.) from the sun roof housing.
- Remove the four wheels.
Op. 4450B14 WHEELS (FOUR) - R+R [→939010877]
Op. 7055B44 LEFT REAR WHEEL ARCH LINER - R.R [→939011361]
Op. 7055B45 RIGHT REAR WHEEL ARCH LINER - R.R [→939011362]
Op. 7055B90 LEFT FRONT WHEEL ARCH LINER - R.R. [→939011363]
Op. 7055B91 RIGHT FRONT WHEEL ARCH LINER - R.R. [→939011364]
1. Working behind the front wheels, blow through with compressed air in the lower ends (1a) of the front drainage pipes (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004794.jpg]

1. Working behind the rear wheels, blow through with compressed air in the lower ends (1a) of the rear drainage pipes (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004795.jpg]

1. Remove any extraneous material from the channels (1a) and the drainage openings (1b) in the sun roof housing.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004796.jpg]

- Introduce small amounts of water into the drainage openings and channels and check that it comes out of the pipes.

Op. 7055B90 LEFT FRONT WHEEL ARCH LINER - R.R. [→939011363]
Op. 7055B45 RIGHT REAR WHEEL ARCH LINER - R.R [→939011362]
Op. 7055B44 LEFT REAR WHEEL ARCH LINER - R.R [→939011361]
Op. 4450B14 WHEELS (FOUR) - R+R [→939010877]
Op. 7030B18 SLIDING ROOF GLASS PANEL - R+R [→939004826]

[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004797.jpg]

1. If necessary, release the end of travel block (1a) from its housing, then release the channel drive rod (1b) and release the channel pad (1c) from the guide.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004798.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the channel is not damaged.
- If removed, insert the channel pad in the guide, then attach the drive rod for the channel and fit the end of travel block in place.
- Position the channel and attach the retainers on both sides.

## 7030B42 SLIDING ROOF SPOILER - R+R  [939004832]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7030 SUN ROOF/GLASS PANEL > 7030B SLIDING ROOF > 7030B42 SLIDING ROOF SPOILER - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004832

##### Removing
- Retract the transparent panel until the spoiler fastening bolt (1) can be accessed.
1. Undo the spoiler retaining bolt on both sides.
2. Remove the spoiler (2a) releasing the side retainers (2b) from the seats (2c).
3. If necessary, release the spring and support.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004799.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the spoiler is not damaged.
- If removed, fit the spring and support.
- Position the spoiler, fitting the retainers in their housings, then tighten the bolts.
- Check that the spoiler is working by activating the sun roof, opening and closing it.

## 7030B54 SUN ROOF (ASSEMBLY) - ALIGNMENT AND POSITIONING  [939004834]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7030 SUN ROOF/GLASS PANEL > 7030B SLIDING ROOF > 7030B54 SUN ROOF (ASSEMBLY) - ALIGNMENT AND POSITIONING
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004834

- SLIDE ALIGNMENT CHECK.
- If there are no problems that adversely affect operation, place the sun roof in the closed position.
Op. 7030B18 SLIDING ROOF GLASS PANEL - R+R [→939004826]
1. Simultaneously check the alignment of the feedthrough opening (1c) with the blind opening (1d) on the slides (1a) and (1b) on both sides of the sun roof; carry out the check using a pin (1e) with the same diameter as the openings. If the result is negative, align the slides (1a) and (1b) as described below.
- SLIDE ALIGNMENT.
Op. 7040E40 MODULAR FRONT COURTESY LIGHT - R R [→939011255]
Op. 7030B70 SUN ROOF MOVEMENT MOTOR - R+R [→939004837]
2. Manually position the main slides (2a) in the forwards end of travel position, then move the slides (2b) on both sides forwards until the feedthrough opening (1c) is aligned with the blind opening (1d); carry out the check using a pin (1e).
- Fit the control motor in these conditions.
Op. 7030B70 SUN ROOF MOVEMENT MOTOR - R+R [→939004837]
> Note: If there is a break in the motor electrical power supply whilst the sun roof is working, the control unit will stop.
See descriptions 7030 SUN ROOF/GLASS PANEL [→939003546 7030 SUN ROOF/GLASS PANEL]
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004803.jpg]

Op. 7030B18 SLIDING ROOF GLASS PANEL - R+R [→939004826]
- Check that the sun roof is working properly.

## 7030B64 SUN ROOF CONTROL SWITCH - R+R  [939004835]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7030 SUN ROOF/GLASS PANEL > 7030B SLIDING ROOF > 7030B64 SUN ROOF CONTROL SWITCH - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004835

##### Removing
1. Move the front courtesy light assembly with sun roof switch (1a), releasing the retainers (1b).
2. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| SUN ROOF SWITCH | See H060 SUN ROOF SWITCH [→939004091] |
3. Disconnect the sun roof switch casing (3a) from the front courtesy light (3b) releasing the retainers (3c) and the attachments (3d).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004804.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the sun roof switch is not damaged.
- Connect the sun roof switch to the front courtesy light, engaging the attachments and the retainers.
- Connect the electrical connection.
- Position the front courtesy light assembly and sun roof switch and attach the retainers.
- Check that the dismantled parts are working properly.

## 7030B70 SUN ROOF MOVEMENT MOTOR - R+R  [939004837]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7030 SUN ROOF/GLASS PANEL > 7030B SLIDING ROOF > 7030B70 SUN ROOF MOVEMENT MOTOR - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004837

##### Removing
Op. 7040E40 MODULAR FRONT COURTESY LIGHT - R R [→939011255]
1. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| SUN ROOF COUPLING | See D045 SUN ROOF COUPLING [→939003927] |
2. Undo the bolts.
3. Remove the sun roof movement motor and control unit (3a), releasing the toothed wheel (3b) from the slide drive cables (3c) making sure not to slide the actual cables. (If the cables do not slide simultaneously then this could cause the non alignment of the slides resulting in a malfunction of the sun roof).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004805.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the motor and control unit are not damaged.
1. Position the motor and control unit (1a) and attach the motor toothed wheel (1b) to the slide drive cables (1c) taking care not to slide the actual cables. (If the cables do not slide simultaneously then this could cause the non alignment of the slides resulting in a malfunction of the sun roof).
2. Fasten the motor and control unit using the bolts.
3. Connect the electrical connection for the motor and control unit.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004806.jpg]

Op. 7040E40 MODULAR FRONT COURTESY LIGHT - R R [→939011255]
- Check that the sun roof is working.
- If necessary, align the sun roof.
Op. 7030B54 SUN ROOF (ASSEMBLY) - ALIGNMENT AND POSITIONING [→939004834]
- If necessary, reprogramme the control unit: see the "Description and Operation" chapter.
See descriptions 7030 SUN ROOF/GLASS PANEL [→939003546 7030 SUN ROOF/GLASS PANEL]

## 7035 BODY WINDOW GLASSES  [939011345]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7035 BODY WINDOW GLASSES
- Kind: chapter (index of subtopics)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods

- Subtopics: INTRODUCTION - BODYSHELL WINDOWS [939011350]; 7035A WINDSCREEN [939011346]; 7035B REAR WINDSCREEN [939011348]; 7035C PASSENGER COMPARTMENT SIDE FIXED WINDOWS [939005449]

## INTRODUCTION - BODYSHELL WINDOWS  [939011350]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7035 BODY WINDOW GLASSES > INTRODUCTION - BODYSHELL WINDOWS
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011350

#### WARNINGS
Work carefully on the fittings and use the recommended tools correctly to prevent damage.
Use the specialist equipment for removing the window glasses, working very carefully to avoid damaging the glass and the bodyshell.
During the operations illustrated below always use either the piano wire or the vibrating blade to cut the sealant. The two tools can usually be used interchangeably.
Follow meticulously the manufacturer's recommendations for sealants, as far as the instructions for usage and times are concerned.

#### PRECAUTIONS
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.
Use the specialist equipment for removing the window glasses, working very carefully to avoid personal injury.
Always move the glass with the help of an assistant.

## 7035A WINDSCREEN  [939011346]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7035 BODY WINDOW GLASSES > 7035A WINDSCREEN
- Kind: chapter (index of subtopics)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011346
- Subtopics: 7035A04 WINDSCREEN - ELIMINATE LEAKS [939004934]; 7035A18 WINDSCREEN GLASS - R.R INCLUDING CLEANING HOUSING [939011347]

## 7035A04 WINDSCREEN - ELIMINATE LEAKS  [939004934]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7035 BODY WINDOW GLASSES > 7035A WINDSCREEN > 7035A04 WINDSCREEN - ELIMINATE LEAKS
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004934

- Wet the outer part of the windscreen glass using soapy water.
1. Blow through with compressed air from the inside and identify any penetration points checking along the entire perimeter of the glass.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/946000893.jpg]

- If the penetration point is limited in size, seal the area involved from the inside after having removed the components hindering access to the area to be treated.
- Wait for it to dry and repeat the test.
- If the penetration point is more extensive, carry out the following operation.
Op. 7035A18 WINDSCREEN GLASS - R.R INCLUDING CLEANING HOUSING [→939011347]

## 7035A18 WINDSCREEN GLASS - R.R INCLUDING CLEANING HOUSING  [939011347]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7035 BODY WINDOW GLASSES > 7035A WINDSCREEN > 7035A18 WINDSCREEN GLASS - R.R INCLUDING CLEANING HOUSING
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011347

##### Removing
For the left and right sides.
Op. 5050B34 WINDSCREEN WIPER ARM/S COMPLETE WITH BLADE - R+R [→939010947]
Op. 7025A64 ENGINE COMPARTMENT GASKET - R.R. [→939011203]
Op. 7210E22 R.R. WINDSCREEN BASE TRIM [→939011397]
Op. 7040H30 FRONT PILLAR TRIM (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011263]
Op. 7065C68 INTERIOR REAR VIEW MIRROR - R+R [→939011373]
For the left and right sides
Op. 7065C34 SUN BLIND (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011374]
Op. 7040E40 MODULAR FRONT COURTESY LIGHT - R R [→939011255]
Op. 5580E50 ANTITHEFT DEVICE VOLUMETRIC SENSOR R R FOLLOWING ELECTRICAL CIRCUIT CHECK [→939011105]
For the front handles only.
Op. 7065C30 GRAB HANDLE (ONE) LEFT, RIGHT OR CENTRE PASSENGER COMPARTMENT INTERIOR - R.R [→939011375]
- Protect the dashboard and wings using suitable covers.
1. Slightly lower the roof lining (1a) and insert two spacers (1b).
2. Remove the windscreen perimeter seal.
3. Apply a double layer of adhesive tape to the body along the side and upper edges of the windscreen.
4. Fully lower the glass retainers.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001944.jpg]

- The windscreen sealant can be cut using a piano wire or vibrating knife; both methods are described below.
- To cut using a piano wire, proceed as described below.
1. Cut a length of wire of about 50 cm then, using pliers, introduce one end (1a) of the wire through the bead of sealant (1b) securing the windscreen (1c).
2. Fasten one end (1a) of the wire to the special retaining tool (2a) located inside the vehicle.
Fasten one end (2b) of the wire to the traction handle (2c) located outside the vehicle.
3. The mechanic inside the vehicle should keep the end of the tool (2a) on the bead of sealant (3) about 30 cm from the point where the wire passes through.
The other mechanic, outside the vehicle, should pull the handle (2c) following the surround of the windscreen and they should cut the bead of sealant (3).
Position a spatula (2d) against the tool (2a) to protect the dashboard trim.
Repeat the operation in sections of about 30 cm until the bead of sealant (3) is completely cut.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001945.jpg]

- To cut using a vibrating knife, proceed as described below.
1. Use the vibrating knife (1a) and blade (1b) and cut the sealant along the side (1c) of the glass working from the outside of the vehicle.
2. Fit the blade (2a) and work from inside the vehicle to cut the sealant strip along all the other sides (2b) of the windscreen.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001946.jpg]

1. After cutting round the entire sealant strip, apply suction pads to the windscreen and then, with the assistance of a second mechanic, remove the windscreen and rest it on a support stand.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001947.jpg]

##### Refitting
1. Using suitable equipment, cut and level the bead of sealant in the windscreen housing leaving a thickness of between 0.25 and 1 mm, taking care not to scratch the paintwork. The thickness of the sealant remaining in the housing will act as a support for the new bonding.
2. Clean the windscreen seat thoroughly using compressed air and degrease carefully with heptane. When bonding, 60 minutes after the bead of sealant has been cut and levelled, the surface of the actual sealant must be treated using the special activating product and it is necessary to wait for 15 minutes before fitting the windscreen.
| Component | Type | Description | Classification | Qty. | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Windscreen glass | Activator for PUR | - | - | - |  |
- If necessary, touch up the paintwork.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001948.jpg]

- New window preparation.
1. Degrease the serigraphed perimeter of the new windscreen using heptane.
2. Apply the glass adhesion promoter (primer) (2b) to the serigraphed perimeter using the wick (2a) supplied in the Kit.
> Note: Wait for about 15 minutes before continuing with the next operation to allow the adhesion promoter (primer) thinner to evaporate.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001949.jpg]

- Preparing a removed window.
1. Using suitable equipment cut and level the bead of sealant on the windscreen so that it is as thin as possible. The remaining sealant does not have to be completely removed; take care not to damage the black serigraphy on the glass perimeter.
2. Clean the perimeter of the serigraphed area of the windscreen thoroughly with compressed air and degrease it using heptane.
Check that the glass is not damaged.
When bonding, 60 minutes after the bead of sealant has been cut and levelled, the surface of the actual sealant must be treated using the special activating product and it is necessary to wait for 15 minutes before fitting the windscreen.
| Component | Type | Description | Classification | Qty. | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Windscreen glass | Activator for PUR | - | - | - |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001950.jpg]

1. Fit the seal on the perimeter of the windscreen.
Use a new seal.
2. Raise the windscreen retainers.
3. Remove the adhesive tape from the edges of the bodywork and along the side and upper perimeters of the windscreen housing.
4. Test fit and centre the windscreen perfectly, using the retainers (2).
5. When the windscreen has been centered, mark the position of the windscreen in relation to the housing using strips of adhesive tape.
6. Cut the strips of adhesive tape and remove the windscreen.
7. Using a spray gun apply a bead of sealant along the perimeter of the rearscreen as evenly as possible.
| Component | Type | Description | Classification | Qty. | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Windscreen glass | Windscreen sealant | Product n° 71711976 | - | - |  |
> Note: Start the operation from the centre of the lower edge and continue without breaks around the entire perimeter of the glass.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001951.jpg]

1. Place the windscreen in the housing with the assistance of a second mechanic immediately after applying the sealant and let it bed in.
2. Position the belt (2a), as illustrated, and tension it so that even pressure is exerted on the entire windscreen to ensure that the matching between the glass (2b), sealant (2c) and windscreen housing (2d) is correct.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001952.jpg]

- Before refitting the vehicle interior and exterior elements, check that there are no infiltration points by carrying out a water leakage test.
- Position the roof trim after removing the two spacers.

## 7035B REAR WINDSCREEN  [939011348]

## 7035B04 REAR WINDSCREEN - ELIMINATE LEAKS  [939004935]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7035 BODY WINDOW GLASSES > 7035B REAR WINDSCREEN > 7035B04 REAR WINDSCREEN - ELIMINATE LEAKS
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004935

- Wet the outer part of the rearscreen glass using soapy water.
1. Blow through with compressed air from the inside and identify any penetration points checking along the entire perimeter of the glass.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005176.jpg]

- If the penetration point is limited in size, seal the area involved from the inside after having removed the components hindering access to the area to be treated.
- Wait for it to dry and repeat the test.
- If penetration point is more extensive, detach and refit the rearscreen.
Op. 7035B14 REAR WINDOW GLASS - R.R INCLUDES CLEANING HOUSING [→939011349]

- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods

##### Removing

For the left and right sides.
Op. 7040L44 REAR DOOR RUNNING BOARD (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011275]
For the left and right sides.
Op. 7050A19 REAR SEAT BACKREST - R.R [→939011324]
Op. 7040H42 REAR PILLAR TRIM (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT- R.R [→939011266]
1. Disconnect heated rear windscreen electrical connections.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| HEATED REAR WINDSCREEN | See P055 HEATED REARSCREEN [→939004602] |
2. Move the rearscreen side electrical connections so that they are not damaged during the cutting of the sealant.
- Protect the rear parcel shelf with a suitable dust cover.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001953.jpg]

1. Remove the window perimeter seal.
2. Apply a double layer of adhesive tape to the body along the side and upper edges of the rearscreen.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001954.jpg]

- The rearscreen sealant can be cut using a wire or vibrating knife; both methods are described below.
- To cut using a piano wire, proceed as described below.
1. Cut a length of wire of about 50 cm, then use pliers to introduce one end (1a) of the wire through bead of sealant (1b) securing rearscreen (1c).
2. Fasten one end (1a) of the wire to special retaining tool (2a) located inside the vehicle.
Fasten one end (2b) of the wire to traction handle (2c) located outside the vehicle.
3. The mechanic inside the vehicle should keep the end of the tool (2a) which is on the bead of sealant (3a) at about 30 cm from the point where the wire passes through.
The other mechanic, outside the vehicle, should pull the handle (2c), following the surround of the rearscreen and they should cut the bead of sealant (3a).
Repeat the operation in sections of about 30 cm until the bead of sealant (3a) is completely cut.
Take care when cutting over the two rearscreen side centering pins (3b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001955.jpg]

- To cut using a vibrating knife, proceed as described below.
1. Use vibrating knife (1a) and blade (1b) and cut sealant strip (1c) along the perimeter of the rearscreen, working from the inside of the vehicle.
Take care when cutting over the rearscreen side centering pins (1d).
2. After cutting round the entire sealant strip, apply suction pads to the rearscreen; then, with the help of a second mechanic, remove the rearscreen and rest it on a support stand.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001956.jpg]

##### Refitting
1. Using suitable equipment, cut and level the bead of sealant in the rearcreen housing leaving a thickness of between 0.25 and 1 mm, taking care not to scratch the paintwork. The thickness of the sealant remaining in the housing will act as a support for the new bonding.
2. Thoroughly clean the rearscreen housing using an air jet and degrease fully using heptane.
When bonding, 60 minutes after the bead of sealant has been cut and levelled, the surface of the actual sealant must be treated using the special activating product and it is necessary to wait for 15 minutes before fitting the rearscreen.
| Component | Type | Description | Classification | Qty. | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Rear windscreen glass | Activator for PUR | - | - | - |  |
- If necessary, touch up the paintwork.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001957.jpg]

- New rearscreen preparation.
1. Using heptane, degrease the serigraphed perimeter of the new rearscreen involved in the bonding.
2. Apply glass adhesion promoter (primer) (2b) to serigraphed perimeter using wick (2a) supplied in the kit.
> Note: Wait for about 15 minutes before continuing with the next operation, in order to allow the adhesion promoter (primer) thinner to evaporate.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001958.jpg]

- Preparing a removed rearscreen.
1. The rearscreen is usable even with the two centering pins cut.
2. Using suitable equipment cut and level the bead of sealant on the rearscreen so that it is as thin as possible.
The remaining sealant does not have to be completely removed; take care not to damage the black serigraphy on the glass perimeter.
3. Clean the perimeter of the serigraphed area of the windscreen thoroughly with compressed air and degrease it using heptane.
Check that the glass is not damaged.
When bonding, 60 minutes after the bead of sealant has been cut and levelled, the surface of the actual sealant must be treated using the special activating product and it is necessary to wait for 15 minutes before fitting the rearscreen.
| Component | Type | Description | Classification | Qty. | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Windscreen glass | Activator for PUR | - | - | - |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001959.jpg]

1. Remove the adhesive tape from the edges of the bodywork.
2. Fit the perimeter seal on the glass.
3. Carry out a test fitting and centre the rearscreen perfectly.
4. When the rearscreen has been centered, mark the position of the rearscreen in relation to the housing using strips of adhesive tape.
5. Cut the strips of adhesive tape and remove the rearscreen.
6. Using a spray gun, apply a bead of sealant along the perimeter of the rearscreen as evenly as possible.
| Component | Type | Description | Classification | Qty. | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Rear windscreen glass | Windscreen sealant | Product n° 71711976 | - | - |  |
> Note: Start the operation from the centre of the lower edge and continue without breaks around the entire perimeter of the glass.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001960.jpg]

1. With the assistance of a second mechanic, place the rearscreen in the housing straight after applying the sealant and let it bed in.
2. Position belt (2a) as illustrated and tension it so that even pressure is exerted on the entire rearscreen to ensure that the matching between glass (2b), sealant (2c) and rearscreen housing (2d) is correct.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001961.jpg]

- Before refitting the vehicle interior and exterior elements, check that there are no infiltration points by carrying out a water leakage test.
- Connect the rearscreen electrical connections.

## INTRODUCTION - BODY INTERIOR UPHOLSTERY AND TRIM  [939011285]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7040 BODY INTERIOR FITTINGS AND TRIM > INTRODUCTION - BODY INTERIOR UPHOLSTERY AND TRIM
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011285

#### WARNINGS
Work carefully on the fittings and use the recommended tools correctly to prevent damage.

#### PRECAUTIONS
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.

## 7040A14 FLAP OF GLOVE COMPARTMENT TRIM ELEMENT (ONE) - ARRANGE  [939011235]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7040 BODY INTERIOR FITTINGS AND TRIM > 7040A PASSENGER COMPARTMENT TRIMS > 7040A14 FLAP OF GLOVE COMPARTMENT TRIM ELEMENT (ONE) - ARRANGE
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011235

##### Removing
For the passenger side.
Op. 7040A65 CONSOLE SIDE CLOSING (ONE) - R.R. [→939011230]
Op. 7040G20 BULB HOLDER FOR COMPARTMENT LIGHTING IN DASHBOARD - R.R. [→939011260]
Op. 7040A31 DASHBOARD COMPARTMENT ASSEMBLY - R.R. [→939011237]
1. Working on both sides of the flap, undo the bolt (1a) and remove the opening clutch mechanism (1b).
2. Working on both sides of the flap, extract the pins.
3. Remove the flap.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000680.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the flap is not damaged.
- Position the flap and insert the pins.
- Place the opening clutch mechanisms in position, then tighten the bolts.
Op. 7040A31 DASHBOARD COMPARTMENT ASSEMBLY - R.R. [→939011237]
Op. 7040G20 BULB HOLDER FOR COMPARTMENT LIGHTING IN DASHBOARD - R.R. [→939011260]

Remove the radio or, alternatively, the navigator.

1. Release the retaining button for the gear lever boot.
2. Lift up the trim (2a) slightly releasing the internal retainers (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000686.jpg]

1. Undo the trim fixing bolts.
2. Move the trim slightly.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000687.jpg]

1. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |

2. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| STARTING BUTTON | See H2 STARTING PUSH BUTTON [→939004057 H002 STARTING PUSH BUTTON] |
3. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |

4. Keep the trim assembly.

Op. 5520G30 START/STOP SWITCH FOR EASY-GO SYSTEM STARTER MOTOR - R.R. [→939011004]
Op. 5505A31 ELECTRONIC KEY ACKNOWLEDGEMENT DEVICE - R. R. [→939010968]

##### Refitting
Check that the trim is not damaged.
If removed:
Op. 5040D18 AIR CONDITIONING CONTROL UNIT - R.R. [→939010931]
Op. 5570T10 CAR RADIO SUPPORT - R+R - INCLUDES CONTACT REPAIR AND SUPPLY CIRCUIT CHECK [→939011074]
Op. 5520G30 START/STOP SWITCH FOR EASY-GO SYSTEM STARTER MOTOR - R.R. [→939011004]
Op. 5505A31 ELECTRONIC KEY ACKNOWLEDGEMENT DEVICE - R. R. [→939010968]
- Connect the electrical connections.
- Position the trim assembly and tighten the bolts.
- Position the gear lever surrounding trim and attach the retainers.
- Fit the boot retaining button.

Op. 5010C34 AIR OUTLET ON CONSOLE - R+R [→939004909]

## 7040A26 STEERING COLUMN TRIM - R+R  [939011231]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7040 BODY INTERIOR FITTINGS AND TRIM > 7040A PASSENGER COMPARTMENT TRIMS > 7040A26 STEERING COLUMN TRIM - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011231

##### Removing
1. Undo the bolts.
2. Remove the lower steering column trim.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001919.jpg]

1. Undo the bolts.
2. Move the upper steering column trim.
3. Separate the rigid section (3a) of the trim from the flexible section (3b) releasing the retainers (3c).
- If necessary, remove the flexible part of the trim.
Op. 7040A36 STEERING COLUMN FLEXIBLE TRIM - R.R. [→939011246]
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001920.jpg]

##### Refitting
Op. 7040A36 STEERING COLUMN FLEXIBLE TRIM - R.R. [→939011246]
- Check that the steering column trim is not damaged.
- Connect the rigid section of the upper trim to the flexible section, attaching the retainers.
- Position the upper trim and tighten the bolts.
- Position the lower trim and tighten the bolts.

## 7040A32 GLOVE COMPARTMENT OPENING HANDLE - R.R  [939011238]

- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011238

##### Removing
- Open the compartment cover.

## 7040A36 STEERING COLUMN FLEXIBLE TRIM - R.R.  [939011246]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7040 BODY INTERIOR FITTINGS AND TRIM > 7040A PASSENGER COMPARTMENT TRIMS > 7040A36 STEERING COLUMN FLEXIBLE TRIM - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011246

##### Removing
Op. 7040A26 STEERING COLUMN TRIM - R+R [→939011231]
> Note: Follow the safety instructions for this operation meticulously
Op. 5580C18 AIR BAG MODULE - R+R [→939011097]
Op. 4110A10 STEERING WHEEL - R+R FOR REPLACEMENT OR CHECKS [→939010817]
Op. 5550A10 STALK UNIT ASSEMBLY - R+R [→939011059]
Op. 5580C23 DRIVE SIDE KNEE PROTECTION AIR BAG MODULE R. R. [→939011091]
Op. 5560B10 CONTROL PANEL - R+R [→939011063]
1. Unscrew collar bolts (1a), then lower steering column (1b).
2. Use a drill to remove rivets (2a) securing the flexible trim (2b) to the dashboard trim.
Use a vacuum cleaner to recover material removed using the drill.
3. Recover the flexible trim.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001926.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the flexible trim is not damaged.
- Position the flexible trim and apply rivets.
- Raise the steering column and tighten the collar bolts.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |

## 7040A37 FUSE BOX COVER IN INSTRUMENT PANEL - R.R  [939011243]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7040 BODY INTERIOR FITTINGS AND TRIM > 7040A PASSENGER COMPARTMENT TRIMS > 7040A37 FUSE BOX COVER IN INSTRUMENT PANEL - R.R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011243

##### Removing
1. Undo the bolt (1c) and open the fuse access cover (1a), releasing the retainers (1b).
2. Release the pins (2a) and extract them, keeping the cover (1a).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004807.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the cover is not damaged.
- Position the cover.
- Fit the pins and attach them.
- Close the cover and attach the retainers.
- Tighten the bolt (1c).

- Proceed as follows for the driver's side moulding.

##### Refitting
- Check that the moulding is not damaged.
- Refit the moulding and attach the retainers.
- Close the glove compartment lid or, alternatively, the fuse box cover.

## 7040B10 LOAD PLATFORM (LUGGAGE PLATFORM) TRIM - R+R  [939011248]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7040 BODY INTERIOR FITTINGS AND TRIM > 7040B LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT AND LOAD PLATFORM TRIMS > 7040B10 LOAD PLATFORM (LUGGAGE PLATFORM) TRIM - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011248

##### Removing
- Open the boot lid.
1. Release the rear trim lower retaining buttons using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000689.jpg]

1. Lift up the lower edge of the rear trim slightly.
2. Release the load carrying compartment trim retaining buttons using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
3. Remove the load carrying compartment trim.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000690.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the load carrying compartment trim is not damaged.
- Place the load carrying compartment lining in position.
- Attach the retaining buttons.
- Place the lower edge of the rear trim in position.
- Attach the retaining buttons.
- Close the boot lid.

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Station Wagon
##### Removing
- Open the tailgate.
1. Remove the retaining buttons (1a) using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
2. Remove the load carrying compartment trim.
3. If necessary, remove the protection (3a) releasing the internal retainers (3b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005279.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Station Wagon
##### Refitting
- Check that the load carrying compartment trim is not damaged.
- If removed, position the protection and attach the inner retainers.
- Position the load carrying compartment cover and fasten it using the retaining buttons.
- Close the tailgate.

## 7040B17 LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT/LOAD PLATFORM SIDE TRIM FLAP (ONE), LH OR RH REPLACE  [939005452]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7040 BODY INTERIOR FITTINGS AND TRIM > 7040B LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT AND LOAD PLATFORM TRIMS > 7040B17 LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT/LOAD PLATFORM SIDE TRIM FLAP (ONE), LH OR RH REPLACE

- Open the tailgate.
1. Lower the handle (1a) and open the flap (1b).
2. Release the lower retainers (2a) and keep the flap (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005280.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the flap is not damaged.
- Attach the lower retainers for the flap.
- Close the flap and attach the handle.
- Close the tailgate.

## 7040B26 LOAD PLATFORM COVER BLIND - R+R  [939005453]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7040 BODY INTERIOR FITTINGS AND TRIM > 7040B LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT AND LOAD PLATFORM TRIMS > 7040B26 LOAD PLATFORM COVER BLIND - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Station Wagon
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005453

##### Removing
- Open the tailgate.
1. Release the load carrying compartment blind and rewind it.
2. Working on the right side of the blind, raise the lever (2a) and release the retainer (2b) from the housing (2c).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005283.jpg]

1. Working on the left side of the blind, release the retainer (1a) from the housing (1b) and keep the load carrying compartment blind (1c).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005284.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the covering blind for the load carrying compartment is not damaged.
- Working on the left side of the blind, attach the retainer in place.
- Working on the right side of the blind, raise the lever then release it attaching the retainer in position.
- If necessary, extract the blind and attach it.
- Close the tailgate.

##### Removing
- Open the door.
1. Extract the courtesy light (1a) releasing the retainer (1b).
2. Disconnect the electrical connection for the courtesy light.

| --- | --- |

1. Working via the front courtesy light housing, undo the bolts fixing the roof lining.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000697.jpg]

1. Release the rear retaining buttons using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
2. Working via the rear courtesy light housing, undo the fixing nut, then support the roof lining.
3. Pull the roof lining (3a) forwards and release the rear retainers (3b) from the housings (3c) for the roof lining.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000698.jpg]

1. Remove the roof lining extracting it via the left rear door housing.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000699.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the roof lining is not damaged.
- Fit the roof lining in the vehicle, via the left rear door housing.
- Attach the roof lining to the rear retainers and support it.
- Tighten the nut fixing the roof lining in the rear courtesy light housing.
- Attach the rear retaining buttons.

1. Working via the front courtesy light housing, undo the bolts fixing the roof lining.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005288.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Station Wagon
1. Working via the centre courtesy light housing, undo the nut fixing the roof lining.
2. Release the rear retaining buttons using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
3. Release the door housing seals in the area of the roof lining.
4. Release the velcro retainers (4a) and lower the roof lining (4b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005289.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Station Wagon
1. Remove the roof lining extracting it via the tailgate housing.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005290.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Station Wagon
##### Refitting
- Check that the roof lining is not damaged.
- Fit the roof lining in the vehicle, via the tailgate housing.
- Position the roof lining and attach it to the velcro retainers.
- Fit the door housing seals in the area of the roof lining.
- Attach the rear retaining buttons.
- Working via the centre courtesy light housing, tighten the nut fixing the roof lining.
- Working in the front courtesy light opening, tighten the bolts fixing the roof lining.
- Lift up the front seat backrests.
- Lift up the rear seat backrests.

## 7040H30 FRONT PILLAR TRIM (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.  [939011263]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7040 BODY INTERIOR FITTINGS AND TRIM > 7040H ROOF PANEL AND PILLARS LINING > 7040H30 FRONT PILLAR TRIM (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011263

##### Removing
1. Release the door housing seal in the area of the front pillar cover.
2. Release the inner retainers using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000700.jpg]

1. Undo the rod retaining bolt
2. Keep the front pillar trim.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000701.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the front pillar cover is not damaged.
- Fit the front pillar trim in place.
- Tighten the rod retaining bolt
- Attach the inner retainers for the trim.
- Fit the door housing seal.

## 7040H33 GLASSES HOLDER (1) LH OR RH, ON ROOF TRIM - R R  [939004840]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7040 BODY INTERIOR FITTINGS AND TRIM > 7040H ROOF PANEL AND PILLARS LINING > 7040H33 GLASSES HOLDER (1) LH OR RH, ON ROOF TRIM - R R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004840

##### Removing
1. Move the front courtesy light assembly and compartment (1a), releasing the retainers (1b).
2. Disconnect the compartment (2a) from the front courtesy light (2b) releasing the retainers (2c) and the attachments (2d).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004811.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the compartment is not damaged.
- Connect the compartment to the front courtesy light, engaging the attachments and the retainers.
- Position the front courtesy light assembly and compartment and attach the retainers.

2. Lift up the trim (2a) slightly releasing the internal retainers (2b).
3. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| HAZARD WARNING LIGHTS SWITCH | See H020 HAZARD WARNING LIGHTS SWITCH [→939004072] |
4. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| SWITCH UNIT ON TUNNEL | See H035 TUNNEL SWITCHES [→939004075] |
5. Keep the trim.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000712.jpg]

- If necessary:
Op. 7040A62 SWITCH UNIT ON TUNNEL R R [→939011242]

##### Refitting
- Check that the trim is not damaged.
- If removed, position the switch unit.
Op. 7040A62 SWITCH UNIT ON TUNNEL R R [→939011242]
- Connect the electrical connections.
- Position the trim and attach the retainers.
- Fit the boot retaining button.

## 7040L40 FRONT DOOR RUNNING BOARD (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.  [939011274]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7040 BODY INTERIOR FITTINGS AND TRIM > 7040L CABIN FLOOR COVERING > 7040L40 FRONT DOOR RUNNING BOARD (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011274

##### Removing
- Open the front door.
1. Release the door housing seal in the area of the running board.
2. Remove the running board (2a), lifting it up at the back to release the retainers (2b), then pushing it forwards to release the retainers (2c) and lastly moving it backwards to release the front retainer (2d).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000713.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the running board is not damaged.
- Place the running board in position and attach the retainers.
- Fit the door housing seal.

## INTRODUCTION - FRONT SEATS  [939011320]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7045 FRONT SEATS > INTRODUCTION - FRONT SEATS
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011320

#### WARNINGS
Work carefully on the fittings and use the recommended tools correctly to prevent damage. After fitting a cover, mould it carefully onto the upholstery.

#### PRECAUTIONS
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.
The seats are fitted with side Air Bags and pretensioners: follow the specific safety instructions
Op. 5580C AIR BAG SYSTEM [→939011086]

## 7045A10 FRONT SEAT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.  [939011288]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7045 FRONT SEATS > 7045A FRONT SEAT ASSEMBLY > 7045A10 FRONT SEAT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011288

##### Removing
> Note: Before removing and refitting the seat the following preliminary operations must be carried out.
- Extract the electronic key.
- Disconnect the battery leads and insulate them.
- Wait for 10 minutes before continuing.
> Note: FOLLOW ALL THE RECOMMENDED SAFETY INSTRUCTIONSOp. 5580C AIR BAG SYSTEM [→939011086]
- Move the seat to the front end of its travel.
1. Undo the bolt fixing the end of the front seat belt on the seat.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000724.jpg]

1. Unscrew the rear fixing bolts.
2. Disconnect the electrical connections (2a) and (2b).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| DRIVER'S SEAT COUPLING | See D070 DRIVER'S SEAT COUPLING [→939003943] |
| PASSENGER SEAT COUPLING | See D071 PASSENGER SEAT COUPLING [→939003948] |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005480.jpg]

1. Raise seat (1a) at the rear and release pins (1b) from their seats.
2. Release front seat retainers (2a) from their seats (2b) in the floor.
3. Recover the seat.
> Note: Move the seat by grasping it only in the area of the head restraint and on the front side of the cushion.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000726.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the seat is not damaged.
> Note: It is absolutely forbidden to fit seats with pretensioners or side air bags that have fallen or been subject to abnormal impacts.Fit the seat with caution, avoiding falls or impact.Make sure that the battery is disconnected.
- Engage the front seat retainers in their seat on the floor.
1. Connect the electrical connections.
2. Tighten the rear fixing bolts.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| SEAT GUIDE FASTENING | Bolt | M10x35 | 2.2 ÷ 2.6 |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005481.jpg]

1. Position the end of the front seat belt (1a) on the front seat and tighten the bolt (1b) to the recommended torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| FRONT SEAT BELT END | Bolt | M10 | 3.6 ÷ 4.4 |  |
- Check that the seat is working.
- Use an Examiner to check that the system has not acquired errors during the operation.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000728.jpg]

## 7045A24 FRONT SEAT BACKREST ANGLE ADJUSTMENT KNOB (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.  [939011289]

- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011289

##### Removing
- Place the seat in a suitable position.
1. Remove the cover.
2. Remove the knob (2a) releasing the internal retainers (2b) from seats (2c).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004151.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the knob is not damaged.
- Insert the knob, doing up the retainers in the seats.
- Fit the cover.
- Place the seat in a middle position.

## 7045A25 FRONT SEAT BACKREST LUMBAR ADJUSTMENT KNOB (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.  [939011290]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7045 FRONT SEATS > 7045A FRONT SEAT ASSEMBLY > 7045A25 FRONT SEAT BACKREST LUMBAR ADJUSTMENT KNOB (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.

##### Removing
- Place the seat in a suitable position.
1. Remove the cover (1a) releasing the retainers (1b).
2. Remove the knob (2a) releasing the internal retainers (2b) from seats (2c), working through slots (2d) using a screwdriver.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005482.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Insert the knob, doing up the retainers in the seats.
- Fit the cover and do up the retainers.
- Place the seat in a middle position.

## 7045A38 DRIVER'S SEAT BACKREST LUMBAR ADJUSTMENT DEVICE - R.R WITH SEAT REMOVED  [939011293]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7045 FRONT SEATS > 7045A FRONT SEAT ASSEMBLY > 7045A38 DRIVER'S SEAT BACKREST LUMBAR ADJUSTMENT DEVICE - R.R WITH SEAT REMOVED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011293

##### Removing
Op. 7045B10 FRONT SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011305]
Op. 7045B06 GUIDE BUSH FOR FRONT SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011306]
Op. 7045A25 FRONT SEAT BACKREST LUMBAR ADJUSTMENT KNOB (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011290]
Op. 7045D30 FRONT SEAT BACKREST COVER, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011316]
1. Undo the bolt.
2. Move the control terminal of lumbar adjustment device (2a) by releasing retainer (2b).
3. Cut the band.
4. Open retainers (4a) slightly and release control cable terminals (4b).
5. Remove the lumbar adjustment device.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004155.jpg]

1. If necessary, disconnect springs (1a) from retainers (1b) and remove lumbar grille (1c).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004156.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the lumbar adjustment device is not damaged.
- If removed, position the lumbar grille and connect the springs to the retainers.
- Position the lumbar adjustment device.
- Position the control cable terminals in their seat and do up the retainers.
- Apply the band.
- Fit the lumbar adjustment device control terminal in its seat by doing up the retainer, then secure with the bolt.

## 7045A42 GUARD (ONE) FOR FRONT SEAT, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.  [939005557]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7045 FRONT SEATS > 7045A FRONT SEAT ASSEMBLY > 7045A42 GUARD (ONE) FOR FRONT SEAT, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Electric seats
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005557

##### Removing
- Raise the seat until it is in the upper end of travel position.
1. Remove front guard (1a) releasing the internal retainers (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005483.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the front guard is not damaged.
- Position the front guard and do up the inner retainers.
- Lower the seat to the correct height.

## 7045A43 FRONT SEAT EXTERIOR GUARD (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R  [939011294]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7045 FRONT SEATS > 7045A FRONT SEAT ASSEMBLY > 7045A43 FRONT SEAT EXTERIOR GUARD (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011294

##### Removing
Op. 7045A24 FRONT SEAT BACKREST ANGLE ADJUSTMENT KNOB (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011289]
Op. 7045A56 DRIVER'S SEAT HEIGHT ADJUSTMENT DEVICE CONTROL LEVER - R.R [→939011295]
1. Loosen outer guard fixing bolts.
2. Disconnect the backrest trim link.
3. Remove the outer cover (3a) releasing the retainer (3b).
4. Disconnect the electrical connection for the heated seat switch.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| DRIVER'S SEAT HEATER SWITCH | See I065 DRIVER'S SEAT HEATING SWITCH [→939004130] |
| PASSENGER SEAT HEATER SWITCH | See I066 PASSENGER SEAT HEATING SWITCH [→939004132] |
5. Recover the outer guard.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005484.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the outer guard is not damaged.
- Connect the electrical connection for the heated seat switch.
- Position the outer guard and do up the retainer.
- Connect the backrest trim link.
- Tighten the outer guard fixing bolts.
Op. 7045A56 DRIVER'S SEAT HEIGHT ADJUSTMENT DEVICE CONTROL LEVER - R.R [→939011295]
Op. 7045A24 FRONT SEAT BACKREST ANGLE ADJUSTMENT KNOB (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011289]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Electric seats
##### Removing
- Raise the seat until it is in the upper end of travel position.
1. Loosen outer guard fixing bolts.
2. Move trim (2a) slightly releasing retainers (2b).
3. Disconnect the electrical connections (3a, 3b, 3c).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| DRIVER'S SEAT HEATER SWITCH | See I065 DRIVER'S SEAT HEATING SWITCH [→939004130] |
| PASSENGER SEAT HEATER SWITCH | See I066 PASSENGER SEAT HEATING SWITCH [→939004132] |
| DRIVER SIDE SEAT MOVEMENT CONTROLS | See H066 DRIVER'S SEAT MOVEMENT CONTROLS [→939004093] |
| PASSENGER SIDE SEAT MOVEMENT CONTROLS | See H067 PASSENGER SEAT MOVEMENT CONTROLS [→939004096] |
4. Recover the outer guard with elecric controls
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005485.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Electric seats
##### Refitting
- Check that the outer guard with electric controls is not damaged.
- Position the outer guard and connect the electrical connections.
- Attach the retainers for the outer guard.
- Tighten the outer guard fixing bolts.
- Check the seat electric controls operate correctly.

1. Working on the lower inner side of the backrest, disconnect the backrest trim link (1a) and cushion trim link (1b).

## 7045A67 SLIDING GUIDE (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT FOR FRONT SEAT - R.R WITH SEAT REMOVED  [939011299]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7045 FRONT SEATS > 7045A FRONT SEAT ASSEMBLY > 7045A67 SLIDING GUIDE (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT FOR FRONT SEAT - R.R WITH SEAT REMOVED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011299

##### Removing
- Only for outer guide.
Op. 7045A63 FRONT SEAT GUIDE OUTER GUARD (ONE), LH OR RH - R R [→939011297]
- Only for inner guide.
1. Move lever (1a) in the arrowed direction and release terminal (1b) from lever (1c).
- Release sheath terminal (2a) from retainer (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004161.jpg]

- For both guides.
1. Undo the bolt (1a) and move support bar (1b).
2. Undo the nuts.
3. Remove guide (3a) releasing it from guide connection bar (3b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939002834.jpg]

- If necessary, unscrew bolts (1a) and remove seat anchorage mechanism (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004162.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the guide is not damaged.
- If removed, position the seat anchorage mechanism and then tighten the bolts.
- Refit the guide by attaching to the guide connection bar.
- Tighten the nuts.
- Position the mounting bar and tighten the bolt.
- Only for inner guide.
- Attach the sheath terminal to the retainer.
- Attach the terminal to the lever.
- Only for outer guide.
Op. 7045A63 FRONT SEAT GUIDE OUTER GUARD (ONE), LH OR RH - R R [→939011297]
- For both guides.
- Lift up the seat sliding control lever and keep it raised, then manually slide the two guides until they are in the rear end of travel position to align them; lastly, release the sliding control lever and attach the retainers for the guides to the attachment teeth.

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Electric seats
##### Removing
> Note: The seat must be raised to the end of its travel in order to work easily on the guides.
> Note: If working on one guide only, mark the position to align upon refitting.
- Outer guide.
Op. 7045A42 GUARD (ONE) FOR FRONT SEAT, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939005557]
Op. 7045A63 FRONT SEAT GUIDE OUTER GUARD (ONE), LH OR RH - R R [→939011297]
1. Undo the sliding guide retaining nuts.
2. Unscrew the lower seat protection bolt.
3. Remove sliding guide (3a) releasing flexible shaft (3b) from the steering control motor.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005508.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Electric seats
- Inner guide.
1. Tighten the bolt (1a) securing the seat belt anchorage link (1b).
2. Release seat belt anchorage link (1b) from retainer (2a), then move.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005509.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Electric seats
1. Undo the sliding guide retaining nuts.
2. Recover the bracket.
3. Undo the nut.
4. Remove sliding guide (4a) releasing flexible shaft (4b) from the steering control motor.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005510.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Electric seats
##### Refitting
- Outer guide.
- Check that the outer guide is not damaged.
- Check that the outer guide is aligned with the inner guide. If necessary, move both guides to the end of their travel (front or rear) to align.
> Note: When working only on one guide, check that the alignment is respected using the marks made upon removal.
- Do up the electric motor flexible shaft and position the sliding guide.
- Tighten the lower seat protection bolt.
- Tighten the sliding guide retaining nuts.
Op. 7045A63 FRONT SEAT GUIDE OUTER GUARD (ONE), LH OR RH - R R [→939011297]
Op. 7045A42 GUARD (ONE) FOR FRONT SEAT, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939005557]
- Move the seat to the front and rear end travel to allow motor position self-learning.
Check that the seat slides properly.
- Inner guide.
- Check that the inner guide is not damaged.
- Check that the inner guide is aligned with the outer guide. If necessary, move both guides to the end of their travel (front or rear) to align.
- Do up the electric motor flexible shaft and position the sliding guide.
> Note: When working only on one guide, check that the alignment is respected using the marks made upon removal.
- Tighten the nut.
- Position the bracket.
- Tighten the sliding guide retaining nuts.
- Position the seat belt anchorage link in its seat, then fit to the retainer and tighten the bolt.
- Tighten the bolt to the recommended torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| SEAT BELT PRETENSIONER | Bolt | - | 3.6 ÷ 4.4 |  |
- Move the seat to the front and rear end travel to allow motor position self-learning.
Check that the seat slides properly.

- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7045 FRONT SEATS > 7045B FRONT SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT/ARMREST
- Kind: chapter (index of subtopics)

- Subtopics: 7045B06 GUIDE BUSH FOR FRONT SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [939011306]; 7045B10 FRONT SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [939011305]

## 7045B06 GUIDE BUSH FOR FRONT SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.  [939011306]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7045 FRONT SEATS > 7045B FRONT SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT/ARMREST > 7045B06 GUIDE BUSH FOR FRONT SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011306

##### Removing
Op. 7045B10 FRONT SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011305]
1. Using a suitable screwdriver (1a) and a mallet, tap collar (1b) and remove guide bush (1c), releasing front retainer (1d).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004164.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the guide bush is not damaged; if the front retainer is damaged, replace the guide bush.
- Place the guide bush in position and attach the front retainer.
Op. 7045B10 FRONT SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011305]

## 7045B10 FRONT SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.  [939011305]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7045 FRONT SEATS > 7045B FRONT SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT/ARMREST > 7045B10 FRONT SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011305

##### Removing
1. Press the retainer (1a) and lift up the head restraint (1b) until it is in the end of travel position.
2. Press the retainers (1a) and (2a) simultaneously and extract the head restraint (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000729.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the head restraint is not damaged.
- Press the retainers and fit the head restraint.
- Check that the head restraint is working.

## 7045D19 FRONT SEAT CUSHION SPRINGING (ONE), LH OR RH - R R WITH COATING TAKEN OFF  [939011313]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7045 FRONT SEATS > 7045D FRONT SEAT TRIM/PADDING > 7045D19 FRONT SEAT CUSHION SPRINGING (ONE), LH OR RH - R R WITH COATING TAKEN OFF
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011313

##### Removing
1. Release seat sliding control cable (1a) from retainer (1b).
2. Release retainers (2a) of Air Bag wiring (2b).
3. Release retainers (3a) and (3b) and remove spring device (3c).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004573.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the spring device is not damaged.
- Position the spring device and attach the retainers.
- Engage the Air Bag wiring retainers.
- Engage the seat sliding comand cable into the retainer.

- Connect the backrest trim links.
