## 7045D31 FRONT SEAT BACKREST COVER, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. WITH SEAT REMOVED  [939011317]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7045 FRONT SEATS > 7045D FRONT SEAT TRIM/PADDING > 7045D31 FRONT SEAT BACKREST COVER, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. WITH SEAT REMOVED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011317

##### Removing
> Note: FOLLOW ALL THE RECOMMENDED SAFETY INSTRUCTIONS (see 5580C).
Op. 7045B10 FRONT SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011305]
Op. 7045B06 GUIDE BUSH FOR FRONT SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011306]
Op. 7045A25 FRONT SEAT BACKREST LUMBAR ADJUSTMENT KNOB (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011290]
1. Lower the trim and padding (1a) and release the panel of lumbar adjustment knob (1b) from retainers (1c).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004167.jpg]

1. Disconnect the backrest trim links.
2. Release retainers (2a) from retainers (2b) along vertical sections (2c) and (2d).
3. Disconnect retainer (3a) from crossmember (3b).
4. Remove rear pane (4a) by releasing from upper retainers (4b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005492.jpg]

1. Release retainer (1a) of the upper edge of the backrest trim from metal profile (1b).
2. Press the disconnected edge in the upper part of the seat.
3. Disconnect retainer (3a) by releasing links (3b).
4. Release upper retainer (4a) from frame seat (4b).
5. Disconnect the electrical connection for the backrest heater pad.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| LEFT SEAT HEATER | See O040 LEFT SEAT HEATER [→939004542] |
| PASSENGER SEAT HEATER | See O041 PASSENGER SEAT HEATER [→939004545] |
6. Remove the backrest cover with padding from the seat frame.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005493.jpg]

1. Prise up the trim (1a) by releasing from the padding edge (1b).
2. Use cutters to cut all the front backrest trim retainers.
3. Separate the trim (1a) from padding (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004170.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the backrest trim is not damaged.
- Observe the following precautions.
> Note: Never repair the cover. Only use new covers. Any type of backrest cover that is not specifically designed for this particular type of seat cannot be used under any circumstances.
- Check that the identification make of the Air Bag backrest cover is on the same side as the Side Bag module.
- If a new backrest cover is being fitted, position the labels as described below.
- Remove the removable part of the adhesive label from the new backrest cover. Place a copy of the label (1b) in a special folder containing the vehicle details (model, registration date, chassis number) and the serial number of the old backrest cover. The file should be kept for future checks.
- Fit the detachable part of the original label on the lower part of the seat, where similar labels for the seat and the Side Bag module and the Air Bag wiring can already be found.
- If removed, position the backrest trim on the padding and crimp the retainers using special pliers.
> Note: Mould the cover on the upholstery during the various refitting stages.
- Prise up the trim around the edge of the padding.
- Fit the seat trim with padding on the seat frame.
- Connect the electrical connection for the backrest heater pad.
- Do up the upper retainer on the frame seat.
- Tension the links and connect to the retainer.
- Position the front edge of the backrest trim in its seat and connect to the metal profile.
- Position the rear panel and do up the upper retainers.
- Connect the retainer to the crossmember.
- Connect the rear panel vertical retainers.
- Connect the backrest trim links.
- Attach the lumbar adjustment knob panel to the retainers.

Op. 7045B06 GUIDE BUSH FOR FRONT SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011306]

2. Remove the surround.
3. Release all the trim retainers.

- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005568

##### Removing
Op. 7045A10 FRONT SEAT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011288]
Op. 7045A24 FRONT SEAT BACKREST ANGLE ADJUSTMENT KNOB (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011289]
Op. 7045A56 DRIVER'S SEAT HEIGHT ADJUSTMENT DEVICE CONTROL LEVER - R.R [→939011295]

Op. 7045D21 FRONT SEAT CUSHION COVER, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. WITH SEAT REMOVED [→939011315]
- Separate the cushion cover from the upholstery.
1. Remove heat pad (1a) by detatching its adhesive bands (1b) from the padding, then remove braid (1c) from opening (1d).
> Note: If working from the passenger side, take care not to damage the passenger presence sensor.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005501.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the heater pad is not damaged.
- If the heater pad detached previously is being reused, apply spray glue for plastics to the adhesive bands.
- If a new heater pad is being used, remove the protective film from the adhesive bands.
- Insert the wiring for the heater pad in the opening.
- Place the heater pad in position centering it in the upholstery splining.
> Note: Take care not to damage the passenger presence sensor.
- Bond the adhesive section of the heater pad to the upholstery.

## 7045M32 ELECTRONIC THERMOSTAT FOR HEATING CIRCUIT OF ONE FRONT SEAT, LH OR RH R R  [939005570]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7045 FRONT SEATS > 7045M FRONT SEAT HEATING > 7045M32 ELECTRONIC THERMOSTAT FOR HEATING CIRCUIT OF ONE FRONT SEAT, LH OR RH R R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005570

##### Removing
- Raise the seat until it is in the end of travel position.
1. Disconnect the backrest trim links.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005503.jpg]

1. Release the rear part (1a) of the cushion trim from retainers (1b).
2. Disconnect the electrical connection for the seat heating control unit.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| PASSENGER FRONT SEAT HEATER CONTROL UNIT | See M110 FRONT PASSENGER SEAT HEATER CONTROL UNIT [→939004389] |
| DRIVER'S FRONT SEAT HEATER CONTROL UNIT | See M170 DRIVER'S FRONT SEAT HEATER CONTROL UNIT [→939004394] |
3. Move control unit (3a) in the arrowed direction, releasing from retainers (3b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005504.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the control unit is not damaged.
- Position the control unit and attach the retainers.
- Disconnect the electrical connection for the ABS control unit.
- Connect the rear part of the cushion trim to the retainers.
- Connect the backrest trim links.
- Move the seat to an intermediate height.
- Check that the heat pads operate properly.

## INTRODUCTION - REAR SEATS  [939011344]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7050 REAR SEATS > INTRODUCTION - REAR SEATS
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011344

#### WARNINGS
Work carefully on the fittings and use the recommended tools correctly to prevent damage.
After fitting a cover, mould it carefully onto the upholstery.

#### PRECAUTIONS
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: therefore during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will thereby remain unlocked and free to turn.

## 7050A13 SPLIT REAR SEAT LEFT BACKREST - R.R.  [939011331]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7050 REAR SEATS > 7050A REAR SEAT ASSEMBLY > 7050A13 SPLIT REAR SEAT LEFT BACKREST - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011331

##### Removing
Op. 7050A20 ONE-PIECE REAR SEAT CUSHION - R.R. [→939011325]
Op. 7050A15 SPLIT REAR SEAT RIGHT BACKREST - R.R. [→939011329]
1. Operate lever (1a) to release left backrest (1b), then fold forward to the end of its travel.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004174.jpg]

1. Slightly raise the end (1a) of the backrest and disengage its side retainer (1b) from seat (1c).
2. Withdraw central pin (2a) from bracket (2b) and recover the backrest.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004175.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the backrest is not damaged.
- Bring the backrest up to its position by holding horizontally and then engaging the central pin in the bracket.
- Insert the backrest side retainer in its seat.
- Lift up the backrest to the end of its travel and and attach it to the retainer.
- Check that the fold-down backrest is working properly.

## 7050A15 SPLIT REAR SEAT RIGHT BACKREST - R.R.  [939011329]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7050 REAR SEATS > 7050A REAR SEAT ASSEMBLY > 7050A15 SPLIT REAR SEAT RIGHT BACKREST - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011329

##### Removing
Op. 7050A20 ONE-PIECE REAR SEAT CUSHION - R.R. [→939011325]
1. Operate lever (1a) to release right backrest (1b), then fold forward to the end of its travel.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004172.jpg]

1. Slightly raise end (1a) of the backrest and disengage its side retainer (1b) from housing (1c).
2. Release backrest from centre pin (2a) and keep it.
3. Keep the washer.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004173.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the backrest is not damaged.
- Position the washer.
- Move the backrest, keeping it in a horizontal position, then attach it to the centre pin.
- Insert the backrest side retainer in its seat.
- Lift up the backrest to the end of its travel and and attach it to the retainer.
- Check that the fold-down backrest is working properly.

## 7050A19 REAR SEAT BACKREST - R.R  [939011324]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7050 REAR SEATS > 7050A REAR SEAT ASSEMBLY > 7050A19 REAR SEAT BACKREST - R.R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011324

##### Removing
Op. 7050A20 ONE-PIECE REAR SEAT CUSHION - R.R. [→939011325]
For the left and right sides.
Op. 7040L44 REAR DOOR RUNNING BOARD (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011275]
- Open the boot lid.
1. Release the retaining buttons using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
2. Move the two side ends of the rear trim slightly.
3. Undo the upper nuts fixing the rear seat backrest.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001937.jpg]

1. Working from inside the vehicle, undo the lower bolts fixing the backrest.
2. Raise the backrest (2a) and release the brackets (2b) from the attachment pins (2c).
3. Remove the backrest (2a) releasing it from the seat belts (3a).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001938.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the rear seat backrest is not damaged.
1. Place the rear seat backrest (1a) in position passing it under the rear seat belts (1b).
2. Lower the backrest attaching the brackets (2a) to the attachment pins (2b).
3. Tighten the lower backrest fixing bolts.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| REAR SEAT BACKREST | Bolt | M10x20 | 3.6 ÷ 4.4 |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001939.jpg]

1. Tighten the upper backrest fixing nuts.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| REAR SEAT BACKREST | Nut | M8 | 2.2 ÷ 2.6 |  |
2. Place the two side ends (2a) of the luggage compartment rear trim in position and attach the retaining buttons (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001940.jpg]

- Close the boot lid.
Op. 7040L44 REAR DOOR RUNNING BOARD (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011275]
Op. 7050A20 ONE-PIECE REAR SEAT CUSHION - R.R. [→939011325]

## 7050A20 ONE-PIECE REAR SEAT CUSHION - R.R.  [939011325]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7050 REAR SEATS > 7050A REAR SEAT ASSEMBLY > 7050A20 ONE-PIECE REAR SEAT CUSHION - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011325

##### Removing
1. Lift up the cushion (1a) at the front and release the retainers (1b) from the seats (1c).
2. Push the cushion backwards until the brackets (2a) are released from the hooks (2b), then lift it up and extract it from the seat, releasing the two brackets (2c) from the retainers (2d).
3. If necessary, remove the sound insulation lining.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001941.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the cushion is not damaged.
- If removed, place the sound insluation lining in position.
- Check that the cushion retainers, hooks and mounting brackets are intact.
- Refit the cushion attaching the retainers, hooks and brackets correctly.

## 7050A56 REAR SEAT CUSHION FRAME - R.R.  [939011327]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7050 REAR SEATS > 7050A REAR SEAT ASSEMBLY > 7050A56 REAR SEAT CUSHION FRAME - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011327

##### Removing
Op. 7050A20 ONE-PIECE REAR SEAT CUSHION - R.R. [→939011325]
1. Using clippers, cut all the retainers (1a) securing the perimeter of the cushion cover (1b) to the frame (1c).
Also release the ends (1d) and (1e).
2. Release the cover (2a) from the frame (1c) along the entire perimeter of the cushion.
3. Remove the cushion frame.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000733.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the cushion frame is not damaged.
- Position the frame on the upholstery.
- Fit the cover on the upholstery, along the entire perimter of the cushion. Mould the cover onto the upholstery.
- Fasten one end to the frame, then pull the other end until the cover is taut; lastly, secure the end to the frame.
- Using special pliers, attach the retainers fastening the perimeter of the cushion cover to the frame.
Op. 7050A20 ONE-PIECE REAR SEAT CUSHION - R.R. [→939011325]

## 7050A78 SKI FLAP IN REAR SEAT BACKREST - R.R.  [939011328]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7050 REAR SEATS > 7050A REAR SEAT ASSEMBLY > 7050A78 SKI FLAP IN REAR SEAT BACKREST - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011328

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: One-piece rear seat
##### Removing
Op. 7040B20 BACK TRIM FOR LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT/LOAD PLATFORM - ARRANGE [→939011250]
1. Release the retaining buttons using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
2. Remove the ski flap.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000734.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: One-piece rear seat
##### Refitting
- Check that the ski flap is not damaged.
- Place the ski flap in position.
- Attach the retaining buttons.
Op. 7040B20 BACK TRIM FOR LUGGAGE COMPARTMENT/LOAD PLATFORM - ARRANGE [→939011250]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Split rear seat
##### Removing
1. Operate the lever (1a) to release the left backrest (1b), then fold forward to the end of its travel.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004818.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Split rear seat
1. Remove the rear frame (1a) releasing the inner retainers (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004819.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Split rear seat
- Lift up the backrest to the end of its travel and and attach it to the striker.
1. Lower the armrest until it is in the end of travel position.
2. Rotate the release knob (2a) and lower the ski flap (2b).
3. Release the inner retainers (3a) and move the front frame (3b) slightly forwards.
4. Remove the frame and ski flap (4a) lifting it up to release the lower retainers (4b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004820.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Split rear seat
##### Refitting
- Check that the ski flap and frame are not damaged.
- Place the ski flap and frame in position and attach the lower retainers and then the other perimeter retainers.
- Lift up the ski flap and secure it using the knob.
- Raise the armrest.
- Operate the lever to release the backrest, then fold it forward to the end of its travel.
- Position the rear frame and attach the inner retainers.
- Lift up the backrest to the end of its travel and and attach it to the striker.

## 7050B08 GUIDE BUSH FOR HEAD RESTRAINT (ONE) FOR REAR SEAT - R.R  [939011333]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7050 REAR SEATS > 7050B REAR SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT/ARMREST > 7050B08 GUIDE BUSH FOR HEAD RESTRAINT (ONE) FOR REAR SEAT - R.R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods

##### Removing
Op. 7050B12 REAR SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT - R.R. [→939011334]
1. Using a suitable screwdriver (1a) and a mallet, tap the collar (1b) and lift up the guide bush (1c) releasing the front retainer (1d).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000735.jpg]

##### Refitting

## 7050B12 REAR SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT - R.R.  [939011334]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7050 REAR SEATS > 7050B REAR SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT/ARMREST > 7050B12 REAR SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011334

##### Removing
1. Press the retainer (1a) and lift up the head restraint (1b) until it is in the end of travel position.
2. Press the retainers (1a) and (2a) simultaneously and extract the head restraint (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000736.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the head restraint is not damaged.
- Press the retainers and fit the head restraint.
- Check that the head restraint is working.

## 7050B18 REAR SEAT ARMREST - R+R  [939011335]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7050 REAR SEATS > 7050B REAR SEAT HEAD RESTRAINT/ARMREST > 7050B18 REAR SEAT ARMREST - R+R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011335

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: One-piece rear seat
##### Removing
1. Using special pliers, retract the pin (1a) first and release it from the retainer (1b), then retract the pin (1c) and release it from the retainer (1d).
2. Extract the armrest and keep it.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000737.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: One-piece rear seat
##### Refitting
- Check that the armrest is not damaged.
- Fit the armrest in place and attach the pins.

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Split rear seat
##### Removing
1. Operate the lever (1a) to release the right backrest (1b), then fold forward to the end of its travel.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004821.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Split rear seat
1. Working at the base of the armrest, release the trim.
2. Undo the bolts (2a) and release the circlip (2b).
3. Remove the plate.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004822.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Split rear seat
1. Rotate the armrest by about 45°, then extract the pin (1a) from the housing (1b) and the pin (1c) from the housing (1d).
2. Keep the armrest.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004823.jpg]

> Block applies to: all engines; all production periods; only with equipment: Split rear seat
##### Refitting
- Check that the armrest is not damaged.
- Rotate the armrest through around 45°, then attach the pins in the rotation seats.
- Position the plate.
- Tighten the bolts and fit the circlip.
- Fit the surround.
- Lift up the right backrest to the end of its travel and and attach it to the retainer.

## 7050D21 TRIM OF REAR SEAT CUSHION RR WITH CUSHION REMOVED  [939011338]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7050 REAR SEATS > 7050D REAR SEAT TRIM/PADDING > 7050D21 TRIM OF REAR SEAT CUSHION RR WITH CUSHION REMOVED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011338

##### Removing
1. Using clippers, cut all the retainers (1a) securing the perimeter of the cushion cover (1b) to the metal frame (1c).
Also release the ends (1d) and (1e).
2. Release the cover (2a) from the upholstery (2b) and from the metal frame (1c) along the entire perimeter of the cushion.
3. Remove the metal frame.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000738.jpg]

1. Fold the cushion over and, using clippers, cut all the upper cushion cover retainers.
2. Separate the cushion cover (2a) from the upholstery (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000739.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the cushion cover is not damaged.
- Position the cushion cover on the upper part of the upholstery and attach the retainers using special pliers.
> Note: Mould the cover on the upholstery during the various refitting stages.
- Position the metal frame on the upholstery.
- Fit the cover on the upholstery, along the entire perimter of the cushion.
- Fasten one end to the metal frame, then pull the other end until the cover is taut; lastly, secure the end to the metal frame.
- Using special pliers, attach the retainers fastening the perimeter of the cushion cover to the metal frame.

## 7050D31 TRIM OF REAR SEAT SQUAB RR WITH SQUAB REMOVED  [939011342]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7050 REAR SEATS > 7050D REAR SEAT TRIM/PADDING > 7050D31 TRIM OF REAR SEAT SQUAB RR WITH SQUAB REMOVED
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011342

##### Removing
Op. 7050B18 REAR SEAT ARMREST - R+R [→939011335]
1. Working from both sides of the arm-rest seat, unscrew bolts (1a) and remove armrest pin rotation plates (1b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004176.jpg]

1. Using clippers, cut all the retainers (1a) securing the perimeter of the cushion cover (1b) to the metal frame (1c). Also release the ends (1d) and (1e).
2. Release the cover (2a) from the upholstery (2b) and from the metal frame (1c) along the entire perimeter of the cushion.
3. Remove the metal frame.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004177.jpg]

1. Fold the cushion over and, using clippers, cut all the backrest trim front retainers.
2. Separate the backrest trim (2a) from the padding (2b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004178.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the backrest trim is not damaged.
- Position the backrest trim on the front part of the padding and crimp the retainers using special pliers.
> Note: Mould the cover on the upholstery during the various refitting stages.
- Position the metal frame on the upholstery.
- Fit the trim on the padding along the entire perimeter of the backrest.
- Fasten one tensioning end to the metal frame, then pull the other end until the trim is taut; lastly, secure the other tensioning end to the metal frame.
- Using special pliers, attach the retainers fastening the perimeter of the cushion cover to the metal frame.
- Working from both sides of the armrest seat, position the pin rotation plates and tighten the bolts.
Op. 7050B18 REAR SEAT ARMREST - R+R [→939011335]

## INTRODUCTION - MOULDINGS AND PROTECTION  [939011370]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7055 MOULDINGS AND PROTECTION > INTRODUCTION - MOULDINGS AND PROTECTION
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011370

#### PRECAUTIONS
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.

## 7055A EXTERIOR MOULDINGS  [939011352]

## 7055A10 MOULDING ON ROOF PANEL (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.  [939011357]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7055 MOULDINGS AND PROTECTION > 7055A EXTERIOR MOULDINGS > 7055A10 MOULDING ON ROOF PANEL (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011357

##### Removing
1. Use a plastic spatula to raise one end (1a) of the moulding by releasing from its adhesive retainer (1b).
2. Fully remove moulding (2a) by undoing side retainers (2b) and the other adhesive retainer at the end.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004180.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the moulding is not damaged.
1. Clean area (1a) where adhesive pads (1b) are anchored with heptane.
2. Remove adhesive pad protections (1c) and immediately fit moulding (2b) in place, attaching the adhesive pads and side retainers.
3. Press hard on thethe moulding over the adhesive pads for a few seconds.
- Check that the moulding is correctly positioned.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004181.jpg]

## 7055B44 LEFT REAR WHEEL ARCH LINER - R.R  [939011361]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7055 MOULDINGS AND PROTECTION > 7055B EXTERIOR PROTECTIONS > 7055B44 LEFT REAR WHEEL ARCH LINER - R.R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011361

##### Removing
- Position the vehicle on a lift.
- Raise the vehicle.
1. Undo the bolts fixing the wheel arch.
2. Release the retaining button for the wheel arch using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
3. Undo the nuts fixing the wheel arch.
4. Remove the wheel arch releasing it from its housing.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000745.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the wheel arch is not damaged.
- Place the wheel arch in position and fit it.
- Tighten the wheel arch retaining nuts.
- Fit the retaining button.
- Tighten the bolts.
- Remove the vehicle from the lift.

## 7065 SAFETY BELTS AND ACCESSORIES  [939011371]

- Subtopics: INTRODUCTION - SEAT BELTS AND ACCESSORIES [939011386]; 7065A FRONT SAFETY BELTS [939011383]; 7065B REAR SAFETY BELTS [939011378]; 7065C ACCESSORIES AND DECORATIONS [939011372]

## INTRODUCTION - SEAT BELTS AND ACCESSORIES  [939011386]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7065 SAFETY BELTS AND ACCESSORIES > INTRODUCTION - SEAT BELTS AND ACCESSORIES
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011386

#### WARNINGS
Work carefully on the fittings and use the recommended tools correctly to prevent damage.
Take care to avoid any damage to the body paintwork.
When working on the seat belt pretensioners, refer to the warnings for the Air Bags
Op. 5580C AIR BAG SYSTEM [→939011086]

#### PRECAUTIONS
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected, the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.
When working on the seat belt pretensioners, refer to the precautions for the Air Bags
Op. 5580C AIR BAG SYSTEM [→939011086]
In particular, with regard to the pretensioners:
- Pretensioners are made from pyrotechnic materials and therefore must be moved, handled and stored correctly in order to avoid damage or cracks.
- In normal conditions, pretensioners are only activated on the action of the electric ignition control during a collision. During activation small quantities of gas develop. This gas is mostly made up of nitrogen; this is a non-toxic gas.
- Pretensioner surfaces must be protected from exposure for over 6 hours to temperatures above 100° C, as well as from sparks and from naked flames.
- With temperatures above 180° C the auto-ignition of the gas generator can be checked.
- Pretensioners should be protected from the stress of collisions or falls.
- Pretensioners that have undergone a fall of over 1 metre onto concrete or similar materials, must no longer be used and should be sent back to the supplier, with details of the fault.
- Gas generators must not come into contact with acids, water, grease or heavy metals; contact with these substances can cause the formation of toxic, poisonous gases or explosive compounds.
- Un-burnt gas generator fuel is slightly flammable, therefore components should never be dismantled, damaged, sawed or interfered with.
> Note: Pretensioners have a validity of 14 years : they therefore need to be replaced after this time.
> Note: It is forbidden to store pretensioners with:- flammable materials- combustible materials
> Note: It is strictly forbidden to dismantle pretensioners into their component parts.
- When being shipped in on-road vehicles, they must be stored in the luggage compartment. The device cannot be shipped in the passenger compartment.
- Buckles with pretensioners should be kept in dry places and protected from dampness.
- Buckles with pretensioners must only be kept in cupboards or compartments that can be locked; these must be ventilated and far away from naked flames and heat sources.
- During breaks or at the end of a job, buckles with pretensioners must be kept under lock and key.
> Note: When working on a vehicle (installation, removal) the following points must be respected:- Do not submit pretensioners to heat, for example during welding, brasing, knocks, drilling, mechanical processes, painting with temperatures over 100° C, etc. When carrying out such processes, the pretensioner must be removed from the vehicle.- If heavy knocks are expected to be made in the area of the electronic control unit, disconnect the battery terminals, carefully isolate them and wait 1 minute- Do not carry out repairs on pretensioners.
> Note: - Primed pretensioners that are not installed on the vehicle and are to be scrapped, must first be activated.- Primed pretensioners fitted in the vehicle must not be demolished with the vehicle but removed beforehand.- If a pretensioner fails to activate during an accident, the device should be considered still primed.

## 7065A24 ANCHORAGE LINK WITH PRETENSIONER FOR ONE FRONT SEAT BELT LH OR RH R R  [939011385]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7065 SAFETY BELTS AND ACCESSORIES > 7065A FRONT SAFETY BELTS > 7065A24 ANCHORAGE LINK WITH PRETENSIONER FOR ONE FRONT SEAT BELT LH OR RH R R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011385

##### Removing
> Note: Before removing and refitting the pretensioners, observe the specified safety regulations.Op. 7065 SAFETY BELTS AND ACCESSORIES [→939011371]
- Extract the electronic key.
- Disconnect the battery leads and insulate them.
- Wait for 10 minutes before continuing.
Op. 7045A10 FRONT SEAT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011288]
1. Release retainer (1a) and remove connector cover (1b), moving it in the direction shown by the arrow.
2. Detach the electrical connection for the anchorage link with pretensioner.
3. Undo the bolt.
4. Move anchorage link with pretensioner (4a), releasing retainer (4b).
5. Release the wiring retainers.
6. Cut the wiring retaining bands.
7. Recover the anchorage link with pretensioner and braid assembly.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004185.jpg]

- Place the pretensioner in the special cupboard, resting it on a free surface.

##### Refitting
- Check that the anchorage link with pretensioner assembly is not damaged.
1. Position the anchorage link with pretensioner engaging the retainer.
2. Tighten the bolt.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| SEAT BELT PRETENSIONER | Bolt | - | 3.6 ÷ 4.4 |  |
> Note: Do not use percussion screwdrivers
3. Secure the electrical connection for the anchorage link with pretensioner.
4. Place the connector cover back in position and engage the retainer.
5. Fit the retaining bands on the wiring.
6. Engage the wiring retainers.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004186.jpg]

Op. 7045A10 FRONT SEAT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011288]
- Use an Examiner to check that the system has not acquired errors during the operation.

##### Removing
Op. 7045A10 FRONT SEAT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011288]

[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005515.jpg]

Op. 7045A24 FRONT SEAT BACKREST ANGLE ADJUSTMENT KNOB (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011289]
Op. 7045A56 DRIVER'S SEAT HEIGHT ADJUSTMENT DEVICE CONTROL LEVER - R.R [→939011295]
Op. 7045A43 FRONT SEAT EXTERIOR GUARD (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R [→939011294]
Op. 7045D21 FRONT SEAT CUSHION COVER, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. WITH SEAT REMOVED [→939011315]
- Separate the cover from the cushion upholstery.
- If present, remove the cushion heater pad.
Op. 7045M22 HEATER PAD FOR FRONT SEAT CUSHION, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. FOLLOWING ELECTRICAL CIRCUIT CHECK [→939005568]
1. If present, remove the carpet (1a) detaching the adhesive surface from the padding after having cut the retaining rings (1b) using clippers.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005516.jpg]

1. Remove the passenger presence sensor detaching the adhesive surface from the upholstery.
2. Extract the wiring (2a) for the sensor via the opening (2b).
3. Keep the passenger presence sensor.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005517.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the passenger presence sensor is not damaged.
1. If the sensor removed previously is being reused, apply spray glue for plastics to the adhesive area (1a) after having fitted protection for the sensors (1b).
2. If a new sensor is being used, remove the protective film (2a) from the adhesive section.
3. Place the sensor (3a) in position centering it on the references (3b).
Stick the adhesive section of the heater pad to the upholstery.
4. Insert the wiring (4a) in the housing (4b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005518.jpg]

- If the carpet has been removed, apply spray glue for plastics to the adhesive area, then place it on the padding and stick the adhesive section to the actual padding.
Fit the rings retaining the carpet to the padding using special pliers.
- If removed, refit the cushion heater pad.
Op. 7045M22 HEATER PAD FOR FRONT SEAT CUSHION, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. FOLLOWING ELECTRICAL CIRCUIT CHECK [→939005568]
- Connect the cover to the cushion upholstery.
Op. 7045D21 FRONT SEAT CUSHION COVER, LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. WITH SEAT REMOVED [→939011315]
Op. 7045A43 FRONT SEAT EXTERIOR GUARD (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R [→939011294]
Op. 7045A56 DRIVER'S SEAT HEIGHT ADJUSTMENT DEVICE CONTROL LEVER - R.R [→939011295]
Op. 7045A24 FRONT SEAT BACKREST ANGLE ADJUSTMENT KNOB (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011289]
- Connect the retainers for the wiring for the passenger presence sensor.
Op. 7045A10 FRONT SEAT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R. [→939011288]
- Use an Examiner to check that the system has not acquired errors during the operation.

## 7065B14 ANCHORAGE ROD FOR (ONE) REAR SEAT BELT LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.  [939011381]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7065 SAFETY BELTS AND ACCESSORIES > 7065B REAR SAFETY BELTS > 7065B14 ANCHORAGE ROD FOR (ONE) REAR SEAT BELT LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.

- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011381

##### Removing
Op. 7050A20 ONE-PIECE REAR SEAT CUSHION - R.R. [→939011325]
1. Undo the nut for the right or left rod involved.
2. Remove the right mounting rod (2a) and separate it from the centre mounting rod (2b), or remove the left mounting rod (2c) and separate it from the end of the middle seat belt (2d).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000757.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the mounting rod is not damaged.
1. Connect the right mounting rod (1a) to the centre mounting rod (1b), or the left mounting rod (1c) to the end of the middle seat belt (1d), the place it in position.
2. Screw in the nut and tighten it to the recommended torque.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| REAR SEAT BELT MOUNTING ROD | Nut | M12 | 3.6 ÷ 4.4 |  |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000758.jpg]

## 7065C19 LONGITUDINAL SUPPORT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT FOR ROOF RACK BARS - R.R.  [939005459]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7065 SAFETY BELTS AND ACCESSORIES > 7065C ACCESSORIES AND DECORATIONS > 7065C19 LONGITUDINAL SUPPORT (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT FOR ROOF RACK BARS - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939005459

##### Removing
1. Working at the front and rear of the longitudinal support, remove the cover (1a) releasing the retainers (1b) using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
2. Undo the bolts.
3. Working in the middle of the longitudinal support, remove the cover (3a) releasing the retainers (3b) using the special tool.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1823015000 | Knife | Retainer removal |  |
4. Undo the bolt.
5. Remove the longitudinal support (5a) complete with support seals (5b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005310.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the longitudinal support is not damaged.
- Clean the support areas for the longitudinal support on the roof of the vehicle.
1. Also clean the support seals.
- Position the longitudinal support.
3. Tighten the bolts for the front, rear and middle supports.
| Component | Fastening | dia | Value (daNm) | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| ROOF RACK BARS SUPPORT | Bolt | M6x22 | 0.7 ÷ 1.1 |  |
4. Position the front, rear and middle covers (4a) and attach the retainers (4b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939005311.jpg]

## 7065C30 GRAB HANDLE (ONE) LEFT, RIGHT OR CENTRE PASSENGER COMPARTMENT INTERIOR - R.R  [939011375]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7065 SAFETY BELTS AND ACCESSORIES > 7065C ACCESSORIES AND DECORATIONS > 7065C30 GRAB HANDLE (ONE) LEFT, RIGHT OR CENTRE PASSENGER COMPARTMENT INTERIOR - R.R
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011375

##### Removing
1. Lower the grab handle.
2. Undo the covers and open.
3. Undo the bolts.
4. Remove the grab handle.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001964.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the grab handle is not damaged.
- Position the grab handle.
- Tighten the bolts.
- Re-close and do up the covers.

## 7065C34 SUN BLIND (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.  [939011374]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7065 SAFETY BELTS AND ACCESSORIES > 7065C ACCESSORIES AND DECORATIONS > 7065C34 SUN BLIND (ONE), LEFT OR RIGHT - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011374

##### Removing
- Lower the sun visor
1. Disconnect the sun visor (1a) from the grab handle (1b).
2. Undo the bolt.
3. Move the sun visor (1a) slightly releasing the retainer (3b).
4. Disconnect the electrical connection and keep the sun visor.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| DRIVER'S SUN VISOR LIGHT | See G031 DRIVER'S SUN VISOR LIGHT [→939004044] |
| PASSENGER SIDE SUN VISOR LIGHT | See G032 PASSENGER SIDE SUN VISOR LIGHT [→939004046] |
5. If necessary, undo the bolt (5a) and remove the grab handle (1b) releasing the locating dowel (5b).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000752.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the sun visor is not damaged.
- If removed, place the grab handle in position, attach the locating dowel and tighten the bolt.
- Connect the electrical connection.
- Place the sun visor in position and attach the retainer.
- Tighten the bolt.
- Attach the sun visor to the grab handle.

## 7065C60 INTERIOR REAR VIEW MIRROR FOR VERSIONS WITH RAIN/DUSK SENSOR - R.R.  [939011377]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7065 SAFETY BELTS AND ACCESSORIES > 7065C ACCESSORIES AND DECORATIONS > 7065C60 INTERIOR REAR VIEW MIRROR FOR VERSIONS WITH RAIN/DUSK SENSOR - R.R.

##### Removing
1. Remove the surround.
2. Release rear view mirror (2a) from retainer (2b) by lowering it.
3. Detach electrical connections (3a, 3b).
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| ELECTRO-CHROME INTERIOR REAR VIEW MIRROR | See P065 ELECTRO-CHROMIC INTERIOR REAR VIEW MIRROR [→939004612] |
4. Recover the complete rear view mirror.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004184.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the complete rear view mirror is not damaged.
- Secure the electrical connections.
- Engage the rear view mirror into the retainer.
- Fit the surround.

[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939000751.jpg]

## 7065C69 INTERIOR REAR VIEW MIRROR PLATE - R.R.  [939011376]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7065 SAFETY BELTS AND ACCESSORIES > 7065C ACCESSORIES AND DECORATIONS > 7065C69 INTERIOR REAR VIEW MIRROR PLATE - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011376

##### Removing
- This procedure illustrates the refitting of the plate which, if fastened correctly, cannot be detached from the glass.
> Note: Do not try and detach the plate from the glass because this could damage the glass.

##### Refitting
1. Remove any residues of adhesive from the plate.
2. Thoroughly degrease the mating surface of the plate and the glass using disposable paper and heptane.
3. Remove any residues of adhesive from the windscreen.
4. Thoroughly degrease the mating surface of the glass and the pate using disposable paper and heptane.
5. Apply a suitable length of double-sided adhesive tape (3M 4265, FASSON 450/460 DUPLOMONT 910 F LOHMANN) to the plate.
6. Heat the plate to a temperature of 40-50 °C and place it on the windscreen then exert a pressure of around 5 Kg/cm2 on it; wait until the ambient temperature cools the plate down.
> Note: The rear view mirror should not be subjected to stress for the next 24 hours so that the adhesion is not adeversely affected.
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939001965.jpg]

## 7065C72 FRONT CIGAR LIGHTER SOCKET - R.R.  [939004939]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7065 SAFETY BELTS AND ACCESSORIES > 7065C ACCESSORIES AND DECORATIONS > 7065C72 FRONT CIGAR LIGHTER SOCKET - R.R.
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004939

##### Removing
1. Open the cover.
2. Remove the cigar lighter.
3. Slightly remove the cigar lighter seat (3a) using tool (3b).
> Note: The tool must engage in the grooves (3c) on the cigar lighter seat.
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1876044000 | Caliper | Extracting cigar lighter housing |  |
4. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| CIGAR LIGHTER/CURRENT SOCKET | See P050 CIGAR LIGHTER/CURRENT SOCKET [→939004598] |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/946001018.jpg]

1. If necessary, remove ring nut (1a) by undoing retainer (1b) from seat (1c).
2. Extract the bulb holder and bulb.
3. Remove the bulb from the bulb holder (press-fitted).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/946001019.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the cigar lighter seat, the bulb and the bulb holder are not damaged.
- Refit the bulb and the bulb holder.
- Position the locknut and attach the retainer in its seat.
- Connect the electrical connection.
- Fit the cigar lighter seat.
- Check that the current socket is working.
- Fit the cigar lighter.
- Close the cover.

## 7065C76 REAR CIGAR LIGHTER HOUSING - R.R.  [939004851]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7065 SAFETY BELTS AND ACCESSORIES > 7065C ACCESSORIES AND DECORATIONS > 7065C76 REAR CIGAR LIGHTER HOUSING - R.R.

- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939004851

##### Removing
1. Extract the cigar lighter.
2. Gently extract the cigar lighter seat (2a) using the special tool (2b).
| Tool | Description | Function | Validity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1876044000 | Caliper | Extracting cigar lighter housing |  |
> Note: The tool must attach itself to the recesses (2c) of the cigar lighter seat.
3. Disconnect the electrical connection.
| Description | Component |
| --- | --- |
| ADDITIONAL CURRENT SOCKET | See P051 ADDITIONAL CURRENT SOCKET [→939004600] |
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004834.jpg]

1. If necessary, remove the ring nut (1a) by undoing the retainer (1b) from the seat (1c).
2. Extract the bulb holder and bulb.
3. Remove the bulb from the bulb holder (press-fitted).
[image: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/media/939004835.jpg]

##### Refitting
- Check that the cigar lighter seat, the bulb and the bulb holder are not damaged.
- Refit the bulb and the bulb holder.
- Position the locknut and attach the retainer in its seat.
- Connect the electrical connection.
- Fit the cigar lighter seat.
- Check that the current socket is working.
- Fit the cigar lighter.

## GENERAL REMARKS - MISCELLANEOUS BODY PARTS  [939011390]
- Path: Procedures > 70 FITTINGS > 7095 BODYWORK MISCELLANEOUS > GENERAL REMARKS - MISCELLANEOUS BODY PARTS
- Kind: repair procedure (removal / refitting, operation number = code)
- Applies to: all engines; all production periods
- Web: https://sloeber94.github.io/elrnDB/#/939011390

#### WARNINGS
Work carefully on the fittings and use the recommended tools correctly to prevent damage.

#### PRECAUTIONS
Before working on the electrical system (connectors, electrical components, wiring etc.), always DISCONNECT THE BATTERY.
> Note: If the battery is disconnected the Steering Lock Node remains in the condition (unlocked or locked) that it is in: during workshop operations and repairs, whenever it is necessary to turn the steering wheel with the battery disconnected, leave the TEG inserted when the battery is disconnected. The steering will therefore remain released and free to turn.
